**[00:06:51.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000145)  
At the DTI, our main mandate

**[00:06:53.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000146)  
is straightforward but important

**[00:06:55.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000147)  
and may malaking impact ito

**[00:06:58.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000148)  
sa ekonomiya

**[00:06:59.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000149)  
and sa lives

**[00:07:00.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000150)  
ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:07:02.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000151)  
DTI has three main mandates.

**[00:07:04.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000152)  
Firstly, foreign trade

**[00:07:07.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000153)  
and international investments.

**[00:07:09.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000154)  
Secondly, consumer protection

**[00:07:11.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000155)  
and fair trade.

**[00:07:12.879] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000156)  
And third, MSME development.

**[00:07:14.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000157)  
This is a sector that we definitely

**[00:07:16.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000158)  
cannot ignore.

**[00:07:18.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000159)  
Today we present our budget

**[00:07:20.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000160)  
from a position of economic strength

**[00:07:22.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000161)  
and historic growth.

**[00:07:24.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000162)  
Allow me to highlight our major achievements.

**[00:07:28.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000163)  
For

**[00:07:29.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000164)  
foreign trade and investment,

**[00:07:31.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000165)  
the Philippines continues

**[00:07:33.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000166)  
to position itself

**[00:07:35.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000167)  
as a destination of choice

**[00:07:37.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000168)  
for investments and a reliable

**[00:07:39.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000169)  
trading partner.

**[00:07:40.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000170)  
But we can do so much better.

**[00:07:42.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000171)  
We are currently experiencing

**[00:07:44.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000172)  
an era of economic expansion.

**[00:07:46.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000173)  
In 2025,

**[00:07:48.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000174)  
Philippine exports surged

**[00:07:50.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000175)  
by 15.4%,

**[00:07:52.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000176)  
which is an all-time

**[00:07:57.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000177)  
high.

**[00:07:57.879] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000178)  
We carry this momentum

**[00:07:59.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000179)  
straight in 2026

**[00:08:01.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000180)  
with the monthly export

**[00:08:02.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000181)  
growing consistently.

**[00:08:04.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000182)  
8.7% in January,

**[00:08:07.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000183)  
8.9% in February,

**[00:08:09.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000184)  
20.4%

**[00:08:11.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000185)  
up in March,

**[00:08:13.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000186)  
7.2% up in April,

**[00:08:15.479] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000187)  
7.6% up

**[00:08:17.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000188)  
in May, and

**[00:08:18.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000189)  
24.1% up in June.

**[00:08:21.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000190)  
What does this mean?

**[00:08:22.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000191)  
That the Filipino products are ready

**[00:08:24.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000192)

**[00:08:25.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000193)  
and tinatangkilik na po ang ating mga produkto all over the world.

**[00:08:29.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000194)  
We are very strong

**[00:08:30.879] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000195)  
in the semiconductor industry,

**[00:08:32.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000196)

**[00:08:35.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000197)  
and a lot of

**[00:08:37.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000198)

**[00:08:39.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000199)

**[00:08:41.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000200)

**[00:08:43.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000201)

**[00:08:45.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000202)

**[00:08:48.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000203)

**[00:08:49.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000204)

**[00:08:53.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000206)  
But we have been seeing

**[00:08:55.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000207)

**[00:11:58.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000299)

**[00:11:59.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000300)

The board of investments

approved a total of

**[00:13:37.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000349)  
1.56 trillion pesos

**[00:14:27.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000373)  
So again, with the free trade agreement,

**[00:14:29.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000374)

**[00:14:53.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000386)  
Meanwhile, the

**[00:14:54.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000387)  
Philippine Economic Zone Authority,

**[00:14:56.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000388)

**[00:15:08.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000403)

**[00:15:11.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000404)

**[00:15:19.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000405)

**[00:15:22.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000406)

**[00:15:32.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000407)

**[00:15:36.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000408)  
These projects are projected to generate 27,000 direct jobs nationwide.

**[00:15:42.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000409)  
Through the Create More Act and the 2026 SIPP and the Green Lanes for Strategic Investments,

**[00:15:51.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000410)  
we are...

**[00:15:52.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000411)  
fast and we continue to attract new investors,

**[00:15:56.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000412)  
encourage expansion of existing investments in the Philippines,

**[00:16:01.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000413)  
and we provide more opportunities for them.

**[00:16:04.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000414)  
We make sure that we get things done,

**[00:16:06.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000415)  
and we keep an open line with all the companies that have investments in the country.

**[00:16:14.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000416)

**[00:16:21.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000417)  
So, aggressive po.

**[00:16:22.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000418)  
Ang approach namin dito.

**[00:16:23.959] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000419)  
So, we continue to create more eco-zones to suit the needs of investors

**[00:16:28.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000420)  
that want to invest here in the Philippines.

**[00:16:30.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000421)  
Para may choices po sila.

**[00:16:32.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000422)  
So, every time an investor comes into the Philippines,

**[00:16:35.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000423)  
we present all eco-zones that are available in the country,

**[00:16:39.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000424)  
not just PEZA and BOI, which are under DTI.

**[00:16:42.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000425)  
Lahat po ng eco-zones, we give it to them para makapili sila kung ano yung best para sa negosyo nila

**[00:16:50.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000426)  
or para sa...

**[00:16:52.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000427)  
industries na gusto nilang pasukan.

**[00:16:56.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000428)  
Second is the local industry and MSME development.

**[00:17:00.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000429)  
This is a sector that we definitely cannot ignore.

**[00:17:03.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000430)  
Guided by our first industrialization policy, Tatak Pinoy—proudly Filipino, built for the world—

**[00:17:10.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000431)  
our vision is to elevate local industries across nine key sectors,

**[00:17:15.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000432)  
creating world-class products designed to be sold locally and globally.

**[00:17:21.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000433)  
Of course,

**[00:17:22.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000437)  
Importante ang global market, pero we also need to exhaust the strength of our local market.

**[00:17:27.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000438)  
Kasi actually yung local market of almost 115 million Filipinos is what attracts foreign investments to our country

**[00:17:35.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000439)  
or foreign investors to our country.

**[00:17:36.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000440)  
So we encourage them to export, but we also encourage them to sell locally.

**[00:17:44.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000441)  
Importante din for us to provide market access for them.

**[00:17:49.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000442)  
We have a lot of trade shows.

**[00:17:51.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000443)  
Our trade shows have been...

**[00:17:53.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000444)  
So from those trade shows, we invite now buyers, let's say, hotel buyers, cafe buyers, grocery, supermarkets,

**[00:18:02.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000445)  
institutional buyers, consolidators, and foreign buyers, actually, to all of these trade shows.

**[00:18:09.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000446)  
And our trade show sales has grown leaps and bounds, including those that attend the trade shows.

**[00:18:15.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000447)  
Another thing that we encourage is the double income in a family.

**[00:18:20.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000448)  
So the DTI has also...

**[00:18:21.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000449)  
The DTI has also established the Women's Enterprise Fund.

**[00:18:24.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000450)  
Kasi in the Asian style, it's always the men that works and the women stays at home and takes care of the children.

**[00:18:33.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000451)  
So ang pinush namin ngayon is to encourage double income in a family.

**[00:18:38.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000452)  
So we've set aside 2 billion pesos for Women's Enterprise Fund.

**[00:18:42.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000453)  
So they can do business sa bahay.

**[00:18:44.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000454)  
Or if they have existing businesses that they want to expand, they want to grow,

**[00:18:49.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000455)  
meron kaming fund for them.

**[00:18:50.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000456)  
Later, I will explain.

**[00:18:51.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000457)  
The funding that we have given to, not just the women's, but to other sectors.

**[00:19:00.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000458)  
Okay, when double income, napaka-importante ang double income in a family because we encourage...

**[00:19:07.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000459)  
Because of this double income, there's better education, better quality of life.

**[00:19:11.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000460)  
Pwede na silang lumabas pag-weekend.

**[00:19:14.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000461)  
They can eat out or they can also mag-vacate.

**[00:19:22.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000462)  
Importante rin for them to eat out because tataas din yung mga sales ng indirect investments,

**[00:19:29.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000463)  
which will also provide the jobs.

**[00:19:31.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000464)  
And this will also strengthen the local tourism.

**[00:19:35.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000465)  
So we need to encourage double income in the family.

**[00:19:38.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000466)  
So importante din ito because we really want to push na dito sila.

**[00:19:45.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000467)  
They stay in the Philippines.

**[00:19:46.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000468)  
We want a better life for our Filipino people.

**[00:19:49.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000469)  
So we need to strengthen the small and medium.

**[00:19:51.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000470)  
We need to strengthen the enterprises and we need to strengthen also yung market access for them.

**[00:19:57.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000471)  
In 2025, our nationwide efforts to expand market access, in spite of our budget,

**[00:20:05.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000472)  
we needed to achieve exceptional results and we needed to be strategic in our moves.

**[00:20:10.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000473)  
Hindi siya pwedeng, we just move without strategizing.

**[00:20:15.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000474)  
So we expanded our reach by organizing a total of 33 trade fairs across NDA.

**[00:20:21.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000475)  
NCR and other regions.

**[00:20:23.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000476)  
But we can grow more if we increase our budget.

**[00:20:27.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000477)  
The more market access or the more avenues we have for them to showcase their products,

**[00:20:33.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000478)  
the better it is for them because then makaka-employ sila ng tao

**[00:20:37.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000479)  
and they can also level up the jobs and also help the community.

**[00:20:44.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000480)  
We generated a combined sales of our trade show nasa 1.0.

**[00:20:51.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000481)  
9 billion pesos already.

**[00:20:53.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000482)  
But we are providing wider market access to more than 3,500 MSME's across the country.

**[00:21:00.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000483)  
And for 2026, we will be conducting a total of 38 trade fairs locally, which provide more market access.

**[00:21:09.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000484)  
And these trade fairs are free.

**[00:21:11.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000485)  
So lahat ng mga MSME's or SME's that want to join the DTI's trade fair nationwide, libre po ito.

**[00:21:18.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000486)  
So this is for them.

**[00:21:19.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000487)  
They just have to be government-compliant.

**[00:21:22.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000488)  
Without the market access or place for them to showcase their products, then all these products don't mean anything.

**[00:21:29.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000489)  
The DTI works closely also with institutions such as SM, Robinsons, and other big corporations to actually showcase their products.

**[00:21:41.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000490)  
So naging maganda po ang relationship natin sa mga institution na ito because they're the ones who can really provide for these SMEs.

**[00:21:54.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000491)  
We need to convert their products to sales that will provide more jobs and uplift indirect industries and provide a better life for our people.

**[00:22:04.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000492)  
But we need to be strategic again in our moves.

**[00:22:08.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000493)  
Beyond providing market access through the trade fairs,

**[00:22:11.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000494)  
we are addressing the financial

**[00:22:12.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000496)  
barriers of our local businesses and MSMEs.

**[00:22:15.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000497)  
Ang pinakaimportante sa kanila ay finance, financial assistance.

**[00:22:20.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000498)  
So, we have a lot of loans in DTI SB Corporation.

**[00:22:25.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000499)  
The loans program has already changed since last year.

**[00:22:29.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000500)  
So, up to 5 million, no collateral, and one year, no payment of principal and interest para makausad sila.

**[00:22:37.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000501)  
They need that one year for them to move their...

**[00:22:41.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000502)  
I mean, move the business.

**[00:22:43.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000503)  
Kasi hindi puwede na when they loan, the first month pa lang, nagbabayad na sila ng utang.

**[00:22:48.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000504)  
So we gave one year, basta up to 5 million, no payment of principal and interest.

**[00:22:54.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000505)  
During the...

**[00:22:55.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000506)  
We have 12 billion for this, but actually, we need more funds, especially during...

**[00:23:00.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000507)  
kasi nagka-Middle East crisis.

**[00:23:02.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000508)  
So, during the Middle East crisis, we have established funds that were targeted to address the needs of...

**[00:23:11.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000509)  
of the MSMEs during this crisis.

**[00:23:14.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000510)  
One is the Women's Enterprise Fund, which I mentioned a while ago,

**[00:23:18.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000511)  
where we encourage the double income in a family.

**[00:23:21.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000512)  
Next is the Export Business Expansion Fund, which we have seen growth in our exports.

**[00:23:28.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000513)  
So kung minsan, malaking order nila, wala silang fund,

**[00:23:32.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000514)  
or kulang ang pera nila pangbili ng raw materials or pag-upgrade ng machine.

**[00:23:37.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000515)  
So, we have a fund for them.

**[00:23:39.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000516)  
MSME Business Fund.

**[00:23:41.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000517)  
So natamaan ang mga MSMEs during this Middle East crisis.

**[00:23:45.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000518)  
Then, we have the OFW Negotiation Fund, because a lot of the OFWs came home during this Middle East crisis.

**[00:23:53.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000519)  
So naghahanap sila ng...

**[00:23:54.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000520)  
or may mga negosyo na sila na gusto nilang i-expand.

**[00:23:57.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000521)  
And then, we also have the E-Transport Loan Program,

**[00:24:01.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000522)  
because we encourage the transport operators, the small and medium transport operators,

**[00:24:07.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000523)  
or individual operators, actually, who want to shift.

**[00:24:11.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000524)  
From the combustion vehicles to electric vehicles.

**[00:24:14.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000525)  
Kasi [unclear], nagtaas 'yung presyo ng petroleum, or ng crudo, ng oil, ng diesel.

**[00:24:20.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000526)  
So, we have funding for them.

**[00:24:22.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000527)  
Again, up to 5 million, no collateral.

**[00:24:26.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000528)  
And again, one year, no payment of principal and interest.

**[00:24:31.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000529)  
There are so many that have availed of this loan.

**[00:24:34.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000530)  
So, we are hoping that we can get more funds for this.

**[00:24:39.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000531)  
kasi maraming nag-avail talaga

**[00:24:41.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000532)  
ng ano, ng mga loan—loan programs na ito.

**[00:24:45.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000533)  
Because of the easy payment scheme.

**[00:24:48.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000534)  
And also, we only require, number one, business name registration.

**[00:24:53.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000535)  
Number two, government permits.

**[00:24:56.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000536)  
Number three, bank account.

**[00:24:58.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000537)  
And number four, picture of their business.

**[00:25:00.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000538)  
In other words, we make it easy for them to loan.

**[00:25:02.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000539)  
kasi alam natin kung gaano nila kailangan itong, um, financial assistance.

**[00:25:08.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000540)  
We want to teach them how to fish.

**[00:25:10.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000541)  
And not just...

**[00:25:11.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000542)  
And then, in every calamity...

**[00:25:13.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000543)  
We also have the Enterprise Rehabilitation Fund.

**[00:25:15.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000544)  
Because in every calamity, or let's say there's a typhoon or what,

**[00:25:20.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000545)  
'yung mga businesses natatamaan.

**[00:25:22.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000546)  
So, we need to give them funding so that they can not just physically recover,

**[00:25:26.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000547)  
but the businesses can also recover.

**[00:25:28.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000548)  
So, we also have funding for this.

**[00:25:31.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000549)  
And building on this momentum,

**[00:25:33.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000550)  
we just recently introduced the Youth Enterprise Fund.

**[00:25:37.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000551)  
We set aside 1 billion pesos to encourage the...

**[00:25:40.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000552)  
the youth to get or to explore business.

**[00:25:45.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000553)  
Kasi ngayon, 'yung mga youth are now in the social media all the time.

**[00:25:49.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000554)  
So, their strength in the digital space,

**[00:25:52.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000555)  
they can use it, ah, para maging negosyante sila.

**[00:25:56.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000556)  
So kung minsan may mga produkto sila or they have an idea.

**[00:25:59.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000557)  
At least meron tayong fund para sa kanila.

**[00:26:02.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000558)  
So, again, they can loan from the DTI SB Corporation.

**[00:26:05.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000559)  
Again, up to 30,000 to 500,000, no collateral.

**[00:26:11.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000560)  
And then, one year, again, no payment of principal and interest.

**[00:26:14.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000561)  
So, we encourage the youth to explore

**[00:26:17.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000562)  
and for them to at least know that the government has funding for them.

**[00:26:23.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000563)  
Despite these milestones, the demand from our entrepreneurs remains overwhelming.

**[00:26:28.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000564)  
Currently, we have received, actually, so much applicants now

**[00:26:35.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000565)  
that we need additional finance for this.

**[00:26:41.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000566)  
Um...

**[00:26:42.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000567)  
We figure out...

**[00:26:43.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000568)  
No, we know that napakaimportante this funding because

**[00:26:46.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000569)  
because the MSMEs comprise 99.5% of the business establishment

**[00:26:51.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000570)  
and 62% of the labor force.

**[00:26:53.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000571)  
So [unclear] itong mga negosyanteng ito,

**[00:26:58.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000572)  
then that will give jobs also to our people

**[00:27:00.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000573)  
and will also contribute to the GDP.

**[00:27:05.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000574)  
To prepare industries for the future,

**[00:27:07.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000575)  
we recently launched our flagship AI and

**[00:27:10.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000576)  
scale-up center in our head office, kasi

**[00:27:13.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000577)  
um, we've also noticed that a lot of our MSMEs natatakot sa AI.

**[00:27:18.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000578)  
So, we want them to have at least a knowledge

**[00:27:22.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000579)  
how they can use AI as a tool for them to help their businesses grow

**[00:27:28.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000580)  
and not something that they can be scared of.

**[00:27:30.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000581)  
So, we just launched this together with Converge and other private sector

**[00:27:35.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000582)  
because we realized that government and private sector,

**[00:27:38.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000583)  
together, mas marami tayong maa-achieve as

**[00:27:42.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000584)  
as we continue to work with them.

**[00:27:47.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000585)  
And then we also launched a very ambitious SSF in Butuan.

**[00:27:51.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000586)  
But these are projects that must be done

**[00:27:54.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000587)  
And once it finishes, marami po tayong matutulungan.

**[00:28:00.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000588)  
Kasi sometimes when we get shared services facilities,

**[00:28:03.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000589)  
we only get one machine.

**[00:28:05.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000590)  
And then hanggang doon lang.

**[00:28:06.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000591)  
But sometimes we need to start the process

**[00:28:09.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000592)  
and we need to finish it.

**[00:28:11.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000593)  
So, we have a very big SSF in Butuan.

**[00:28:15.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000594)  
Actually, siya, printing and packaging center.

**[00:28:18.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000595)  
So, if I have a product, then I go to this facility

**[00:28:22.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000596)  
and then I can print it.

**[00:28:24.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000597)  
I can also package it.

**[00:28:27.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000598)  
So pag tapos 'yung project, tapos

**[00:28:30.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000599)  
'yung product, and

**[00:28:30.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000600)  
and then the market access,

**[00:28:32.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000601)  
they can just go to the different regional offices nationwide

**[00:28:35.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000602)  
or 1,400 Negosyo Centers nationwide

**[00:28:39.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000603)  
to find market access for these products.

**[00:28:41.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000604)  
So, we have this for them.

**[00:28:45.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000605)  
And third is the consumer protection.

**[00:28:47.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000606)  
The DTI continues to uphold consumer protection and fair trade,

**[00:28:51.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000607)  
ensuring that our economic gains translate into real benefits

**[00:28:55.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000608)  
for every Filipino.

**[00:28:57.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000609)  
Through rigorous nationwide enforcement,

**[00:28:59.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000610)  
we inspected over 14,000 establishments

**[00:29:03.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000611)  
while maintaining high compliance rate

**[00:29:06.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000612)  
to further protect the public.

**[00:29:08.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000613)  
We closely monitored

**[00:29:09.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000614)  
over 5,000 firms

**[00:29:11.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000615)  
to secure the price and supply of basic necessities

**[00:29:14.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000616)  
and prime commodities.

**[00:29:18.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000617)  
There are a lot of products now

**[00:29:19.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000618)  
that come into the Philippines.

**[00:29:21.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000619)  
So, we need to protect the consumers

**[00:29:23.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000620)  
from the products that are being sold in the market.

**[00:29:26.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000621)  
A lot of them are compliant,

**[00:29:29.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000622)  
but there are some that are also hazardous to us.

**[00:29:32.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000623)  
'Yung iba nakakasunog, 'yung iba

**[00:29:34.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000624)  
nakaka... nakakasira na ng property.

**[00:29:37.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000625)  
So, we need to be strict on this.

**[00:29:39.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000626)  
So, we also need additional funding for this one.

**[00:29:45.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000627)  
Madam Chair and honorable members,

**[00:29:47.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000628)  
the fiscal year 2027 budget proposal

**[00:29:50.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000629)  
reflects our commitment to creating more jobs,

**[00:29:53.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000630)  
attracting quality investments,

**[00:29:55.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000631)  
expanding exports,

**[00:29:57.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000632)  
strengthening Filipino enterprises,

**[00:29:59.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000633)  
and...

**[00:29:59.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000634)  
enterprises, and protecting consumers.

**[00:30:01.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000635)  
DTI programs is intended to generate economic opportunities

**[00:30:05.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000636)  
that extend well beyond the department itself.

**[00:30:08.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000637)  
Entrepreneurs seeking growth,

**[00:30:10.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000638)  
investors seeking stability and predictability,

**[00:30:14.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000639)  
exporters seeking more markets,

**[00:30:16.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000640)  
and consumers seeking protection.

**[00:30:19.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000641)  
Governments build infrastructure,

**[00:30:21.519] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000642)  
enact reforms, and open doors.

**[00:30:24.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000643)  
But you can count on the Department of Trade and Industry

**[00:30:26.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000644)  
to ensure these opportunities translate into thriving industries.

**[00:30:31.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000645)  
We stand fully committed to your progress,

**[00:30:34.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000646)  
and we will execute our mandate as best and as aggressive as we can

**[00:30:38.919] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000647)  
to build a highly connected and consumer-oriented Philippine economy

**[00:30:43.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000648)  
where no one is left behind.

**[00:30:45.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000649)

and sold globally,

**[00:31:00.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000655)

**[00:34:54.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000688)

**[00:35:48.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000707)  
No, Pax Silica actually is just...

**[00:35:52.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000708)

**[00:35:54.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000709)

**[00:35:56.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000710)

**[00:35:59.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000711)  
We have here Undersecretary Perry Rodolfo,

**[00:36:02.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000712)  
who can explain it in detail.

**[00:36:05.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000713)

**[00:36:06.680] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000714)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[00:36:07.319] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000715)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[00:36:08.480] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000716)  
And thank you for the question, Representative Cendaña.

**[00:36:12.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000717)  
We fully welcome this opportunity to be able to provide

**[00:36:15.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000718)  
more clarity on Pax Silica,

**[00:36:18.359] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000719)

**[00:36:22.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000720)  
ang dami ng mga lumalabas na balita.

**[00:36:25.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000721)  
But first of all, talagang we welcome this opportunity

**[00:36:29.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000722)  
and we welcome all of the comments,

**[00:36:31.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000723)

**[00:36:34.400] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000724)  
it really highlights

**[00:36:36.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000725)  
that this can be a transformative economic basis

**[00:36:41.359] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000726)  
for economic policy.

**[00:36:43.119] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000727)  
The more that it is being discussed,

**[00:36:44.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000728)  
kami naman po sa gobyerno, very much open, as I said,

**[00:36:47.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000729)  
negative or positive, because it can only

**[00:36:50.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000730)  
make the policy direction better pagka ho

**[00:36:53.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000731)  
tayo'y nag-uusap.

**[00:36:54.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000732)  
So we really welcome this opportunity.

**[00:36:57.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000733)  
Banggitin ko lang ho na lahat ho ng mga dokumento

**[00:37:00.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000734)  
that we have signed, that we have transmitted to the U.S.,

**[00:37:03.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000735)  
that they have transmitted to us, iyon ho ang mga signed na documents natin

**[00:37:07.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000736)  
ay nasa BOI website as early as April 25.

**[00:37:12.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000738)  
So, pinost na ho natin iyan doon in the spirit of transparency din ho para makita na rin ho ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:37:18.480] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000742)
