**[00:37:31.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000743)  
nasa 790 views na tayo. But we hope that through this hearing, mas marami pa ho ang sumilip

**[00:37:37.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000744)  
sana noong mga dokumento na iyon at saka yung mga video clips.

**[00:37:41.720] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000745)  
Excuse me, Usec. Where is the video again?

**[00:37:43.720] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000746)  
Saan po siya naka-post?

**[00:37:45.200] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000747)  
Nandito po sa BOI website, so boi.gov.ph/discussion-on-pax-silica-and-the-ai-native-industrial-hub.

**[00:37:59.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000748)  
We can provide a copy, Honorable Chair.

**[00:38:03.800] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000749)  
Okay, Usec.

**[00:38:04.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000750)  
And second ho, it's very important to distinguish between the partnership and the project.

**[00:38:10.640] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000751)  
Kasi nagkakalituhan talaga, pag pinag-usapan yung Pax Silica, akala nila iyon na yung ginagawa ho natin sa New Clark City, iyon na ho yung buong Pax Silica.

**[00:38:21.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000752)  
Pero we have to distinguish between the partnership and the project.

**[00:38:25.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000753)  
Pax Silica as a partnership is actually a non-binding statement or declaration that we have signed on to together with now 23 other countries and an economy.

**[00:38:39.400] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000754)  
It is really for securing the supply chain for your AI tech ecosystem.

**[00:38:45.319] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000755)  
Kung halimbawa, ang sinasabi ho nila dito ay, if the past century was run on oil and on steel, this new century will be run on compute capacity.

**[00:38:57.480] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000756)  
And it is about securing the entire supply chain from mining, minerals, to advanced manufacturing, to data centers.

**[00:39:08.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000757)  
To the software that take advantage of this compute capacity.

**[00:39:13.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000758)  
So the whole supply chain po yun.

**[00:39:15.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000759)  
Ang pagdating ko dito sa atin pong project, yung New Clark City po is just one of the projects na pinupush ho ng Pilipinas at saka isang partner country doon sa Pax Silica.

**[00:39:29.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000760)  
It is just one of the project.

**[00:39:31.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000761)  
And this project will really be focused on advanced manufacturing.

**[00:39:36.359] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000762)  
So contrary po sa mga...

**[00:39:37.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000763)  
Agam-agam sa mga concern, we fully understand the concerns.

**[00:39:42.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000764)  
And lahat naman po ng mga sinasabi ng mga stakeholders natin, tama naman po.

**[00:39:47.160] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000765)  
Kaya lang po, yung assumption, halimbawa po tungkol sa kakain ng napakalaking power at tubig, yung mga AI data centers, tama naman po na kumakain talaga sila ng power and water.

**[00:40:04.319] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000766)  
Ang mali lang po ay hindi po iyon yung gagawin sa New Clark City.

**[00:40:09.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000767)  
Ang gagawin po sa New Clark City is really for advanced manufacturing.

**[00:40:13.200] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000768)  
So this would be for the equipment that will go into the AI data centers.

**[00:40:18.079] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000769)  
But the AI data centers themselves will not be in the New Clark City.

**[00:40:23.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000770)  
Be that as it may, kahit ganun po, alam din naman po natin na advanced manufacturing kumakain pa rin po ng power, kumakain pa rin po ng water.

**[00:40:31.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000771)  
Just like any other export and manufacturing zones do.

**[00:40:35.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000772)  
Ang usapan naman po natin dito.

**[00:40:37.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000773)  
Ang basic condition po doon sa mag-develop po ng New Clark City.

**[00:40:43.119] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000774)  
And ito, hindi sa [unclear] ng gobyerno.

**[00:40:47.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000775)  
That you have to build your own roads, bring your own power, and then secure and reuse your own water.

**[00:40:55.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000776)  
So yun po ang usapan natin doon sa mag-develop doon sa New Clark City.

**[00:41:00.720] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000777)  
And isang project lang po yun.

**[00:41:02.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000778)  
Meron po yung whole stack ng AI po.

**[00:41:06.359] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000779)  
Mag-uusap pa ho tayo ng ibang

**[00:41:09.079] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000780)  
Possible locations for those other areas.

**[00:41:12.119] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000781)  
Halimbawa po, pag pinag-usapan natin data center.

**[00:41:15.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000782)  
It may happen in another location na mas appropriate for data center.

**[00:41:21.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000783)  
Ibig sabihin, mas available yung tubig at mas available yung power.

**[00:41:24.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000784)  
But again, build your own power, secure your own water, secure and reuse your own water, build your own roads.

**[00:41:31.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000785)  
Halimbawa po, just last week, kausap po natin isang locality, isang LGU dito sa Manila.

**[00:41:38.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000786)  
Ang pinag-uusapan naman po natin is an IC Design National Hub.

**[00:41:43.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000787)  
So this is more service-oriented.

**[00:41:46.000] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000788)  
Pero pinag-uusapan na natin yun sa isang LGU dito sa Manila na meron na po silang 13 IC Design companies,

**[00:41:54.400] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000789)  
four of which are Filipino companies.

**[00:41:57.000] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000790)  
At meron silang kolehyo doon na ginagastusan din ng LGU na nakafocus sa microelectronics.

**[00:42:05.359] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000791)  
Very important for the IC design industry.

**[00:42:09.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000792)  
And ang kausap po natin na partner na tutulong dito sa IC Design Hub po natin ay hindi ang U.S.

**[00:42:16.160] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000793)  
But another partner which is within the Pax Silica partnership.

**[00:42:20.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000794)  
So iyon ho, iba ho yung partnership, iba yung mga project.

**[00:42:24.119] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000795)  
New Clark City is just one of the projects and it will be focused on the advanced manufacturing.

**[00:42:30.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000796)  
Thank you, representative.

**[00:42:31.599] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000797)  
Thank you, Yusek.

**[00:42:33.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000798)  
Madam Chair, thank you, Yusek Rodolfo.

**[00:42:37.559] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000799)  
Medyo hindi po na-assuage yung fears ng ating mga kababayan sa inyo pong sagot.

**[00:42:42.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000800)  
In fact, sa halip na ma-assuage yung fears, medyo mas naalarma tayo knowing na multiple sites pala ito.

**[00:42:48.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000801)  
Madam Chair, may we direct the DTI to kindly submit to the committee the prospective sites for the other projects under the Pax Silica agreement.

**[00:42:57.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000802)  
Lalong-lalo na po nakaka-bother yung usapin ng use of resources.

**[00:43:02.079] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000803)  
Medyo mahirap pong sabihin na bring your own water and electricity kasi alam naman po natin,

**[00:43:06.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000804)  
pag tubig, doon po magta-tap.

**[00:43:08.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000805)  
Sa atin pong water table iyan.

**[00:43:09.599] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000806)  
So, hindi po infinite ang ating tubig.

**[00:43:12.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000807)  
Finite po yan at kailangan po yan ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:43:15.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000808)  
Let me now go to a discussion on the concerns of the communities.

**[00:43:20.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000809)  
Nakonsulta po ba ang mga community? Kasi napakahalaga po sa atin ang free prior and informed consent?

**[00:43:27.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000810)  
Usec.

**[00:43:29.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000811)  
For the New Clark City project po, based doon sa binanggit na rin po publicly ni BCDA,

**[00:43:38.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000812)  
ongoing po yung consultations.

**[00:43:41.559] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000813)  
So, ongoing ang consultations.

**[00:43:43.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000814)  
Ibig sabihin, before entering into the agreement, hindi po nagkaroon ng consultation.

**[00:43:47.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000815)  
Tama po ba?

**[00:43:50.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000816)  
Madam Chair.

**[00:43:51.520] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000817)  
Usec., please. Thank you.

**[00:43:52.839] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000818)  
On the specific project po sa Clark, the New Clark City is an existing master-planned industrial zone.

**[00:44:01.160] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000819)  
Tapos ho, on the specific na within that industrial zone,

**[00:44:05.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000820)  
yung ating tinatawag na 1,620

**[00:44:08.640] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000821)  
hectares,

**[00:44:09.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000822)  
yun po yung kinukonsulta

**[00:44:11.079] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000823)  
na project. Kasi bagong-bago

**[00:44:13.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000824)  
lang din ho ito.

**[00:44:15.160] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000825)  
Kung mapapansin ninyo nga po,

**[00:44:17.000] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000826)  
pinirmahan lang natin yung project

**[00:44:19.000] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000827)  
na to. And it's a work in progress,

**[00:44:20.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000828)  
by the way, on that new Clark City.

**[00:44:23.720] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000829)  
Ongoing pa rin po yung

**[00:44:24.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000830)  
discussions doon.

**[00:44:26.559] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000831)  
Madam Chair, may we direct the DTI

**[00:44:28.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000832)  
through this committee to submit

**[00:44:31.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000833)  
the status of the ongoing

**[00:44:32.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000834)  
consultations being conducted with the

**[00:44:34.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000835)  
communities. Lubha po tayong naaalarma

**[00:44:36.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000836)  
dito sa nababalitang mga pagpapalayas

**[00:44:39.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000837)  
sa mga katutubo sa kanila

**[00:44:40.839] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000838)  
pong lupang ninuno. Kaya nga po

**[00:44:42.559] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000839)  
nag-file tayo ng House Resolution 1227

**[00:44:45.079] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000840)  
para po imbestigahan at silipin

**[00:44:47.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000841)  
kung ano ang nangyayari talaga

**[00:44:48.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000842)  
on the ground when it comes to the implementation

**[00:44:50.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000843)  
of this project. Let me move on

**[00:44:52.599] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000844)  
to another topic, Madam Chair,

**[00:44:54.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000845)  
and this is on the issue of the

**[00:44:56.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000846)  
DEFA, which was also mentioned

**[00:44:58.359] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000847)  
by the

**[00:44:59.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000849)  
good Secretary. Nagsimula na po ang negosasyon, pipirmahan na po ito pagdating ng Nobyembre.

**[00:45:04.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000850)  
Ang tanong po ng mga concerned sectors, particularly po mga kasamahan natin mula sa SENTRO, isang labor center,

**[00:45:10.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000851)  
would the DTI commit to making the negotiated texts available to Congress and the public before the agreement is signed

**[00:45:16.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000852)  
so that the implications can be properly reviewed and scrutinized?

**[00:45:22.280] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000853)  
Who may answer po?

**[00:45:24.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000854)  
Yes, Honorable Congressman. Yes, Madam Chair. Yes,

**[00:45:28.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000855)  
We will make it available for everyone, and ibibigay din namin sa kanila what are the advantages of this DEFA

**[00:45:37.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000856)  
and what the businesses or the industries can gain from this DEFA.

**[00:45:44.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000857)  
Kasi actually, this is very important kasi we're now in the digital space, digital economy na po tayo.

**[00:45:50.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000858)  
So we need to be able to adapt to the changes of the times and napakaimportante ng

**[00:45:58.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000859)  
the digital space nowadays.

**[00:46:00.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000860)  
And then kasali rin dito yung AI.

**[00:46:03.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000861)  
So we will give a complete and a comprehensive information on this DEFA.

**[00:46:09.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000862)  
Definitely. Sure po iyon.

**[00:46:12.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000863)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Secretary.

**[00:46:14.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000864)  
Hihintayin po namin yung details of the agreement at sana po ma-submit before tayo magplenaryo.

**[00:46:21.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000865)  
Let me move on to the RACE program.

**[00:46:23.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000866)  
Idinulog po ito ng mga kasama natin mula sa Philippine Metalworkers Alliance, or PMA.

**[00:46:30.119] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000867)  
Kasi po doon sa budget ng 2026, ang na-veto na item ay para sana sa restoring the RACE program ng DTI.

**[00:46:38.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000868)  
Ang tanong po natin ay ano po ang mangyayari dito sa RACE program?

**[00:46:42.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000869)  
Ito po ba'y itutuloy pang pondohan ng gobyerno?

**[00:46:48.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000870)  
Madam Chair and Honorable Congressman, yes, matutuloy itong RACE program because marami pa ring combustion vehicles that are being produced here in the Philippines.

**[00:46:59.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000872)  
So we're just finishing some of the final touches of this program.

**[00:47:05.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000873)  
But definitely, matutuloy ito.

**[00:47:07.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000874)  
We're just getting the budget also from the DBM for this.

**[00:47:11.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000875)  
So once completed po ito, we will again inform the public, just like when we had the EVs and all other programs po ng DTI.

**[00:47:21.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000876)  
Ini-inform po namin talaga yung public para alam nila.

**[00:47:24.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000877)  
Madam Chair, may we request DTI to submit to this committee?

**[00:47:27.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000878)  
the details of their proposed budget for the RACE program.

**[00:47:31.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000879)  
Under the Electric Vehicle Industry Development Act, or EVIDA,

**[00:47:36.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000880)  
under the DOE po itong programa,

**[00:47:39.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000881)  
at may sarili din pong electric vehicle program, EVIS naman,

**[00:47:43.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000882)  
I understand ang DTI. Posible

**[00:47:45.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000883)  
po kaya na isa na lang yung EVIS at RACE or any other program sa automotive sector

**[00:47:50.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000884)  
ay sa isang ahensya na lang po ng gobyerno ilagay

**[00:47:52.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000885)  
para hindi po silo. Sayang po yung resources lalo na kung may duplication ng trabaho?

**[00:48:00.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000885-caption-01)  
We will be coordinating closely. But for the EVs, kasi this is the incentive scheme for those companies that want to manufacture electric vehicles in the country, whether hybrid plus electric or pure electric po. So again, we will give you the complete information on this for both RACE and for EVs also. Thank you, Honorable Congressman.

**[00:48:27.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000886)  
Thank you, Madam Secretary. Madam Chair,

**[00:48:28.839] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000887)  
may we ask the DTI kung meron po ba tayong industrial policy

**[00:48:32.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000888)  
which will serve as a roadmap para sa electric vehicle or automotive sector in particular?

**[00:48:47.319] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000889)  
Can you repeat again the question?

**[00:48:48.839] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000890)  
Ang tanong po ay meron po ba tayong industrial policy that could serve as a roadmap

**[00:48:54.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000891)  
para po sa electric vehicle or automotive sector sa ating bansa?

**[00:48:59.839] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000892)  
Okay, you may answer.

**[00:49:01.960] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000893)  
Madam Chair, on the automotive industry po,

**[00:49:04.640] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000894)  
our policy really is to shift towards the electric vehicle

**[00:49:09.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000895)  
as the main technology type for the sector po.

**[00:49:15.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000896)  
Except that hindi ho natin pwedeng pabayaan din,

**[00:49:17.640] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000897)  
kagaya ng nasabi ninyo yung mga nasa metalworking sector ho natin.

**[00:49:21.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000898)  
That's why meron tayong transition towards the full electric vehicle adoption.

**[00:49:27.040] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-000899)  
Nabanggit na rin ho naman ni Presidente doon sa SONA niya yung adoption ng electric vehicle.

**[00:49:32.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000900)  
Thank you.

**[00:49:32.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000901)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:49:33.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000903)  
may we request the DTI to submit to this committee

**[00:49:36.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000904)  
the said policy which will serve as a roadmap for the industry?

**[00:49:39.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000905)  
Yes, Honorable Congressman, we will.

**[00:49:42.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000906)  
Actually, from the CARS program, we shifted lang to the RACE program

**[00:49:45.839] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000907)  
to encourage more companies that want to build manufacturing for the combustion vehicles.

**[00:49:52.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000908)  
That's why yung CARS program naging RACE program po ito.

**[00:49:56.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000909)  
And then to adapt to the changing times also,

**[00:49:59.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000910)  
which is the electric vehicles now, both hybrid plus electric or total electric.

**[00:50:05.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000912)  
Meron ding incentive scheme.

**[00:50:07.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000913)  
This is also, again,

**[00:50:08.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000914)  
to encourage more companies to invest in the Philippines.

**[00:50:12.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000915)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Madam Secretary.

**[00:50:15.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000916)  
Punta na po ako dito sa usapin na mainit na mainit ngayon. Ito pong usapin ng union busting sa DHL.

**[00:50:21.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000917)  
We know that DHL is a major partner of the DTI for MSME support and development.

**[00:50:27.359] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000918)  
DHL Philippines is also a partner of the DTI Supply Chain and Logistics Center

**[00:50:31.559] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000919)  
and was part of the private sector group.

**[00:50:33.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000920)  
convened by the SCLC for the centralization of logistics expertise and industry collaboration.

**[00:50:42.839] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000921)  
Nababahala po tayo kasi ang DHL po ngayon ay may kaso ng union busting.

**[00:50:48.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000922)  
So ang tanong po ng lahat, how can DHL claim to be a socially responsible corporation

**[00:50:54.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000923)  
when it is openly violating the rights of workers in their Sukat warehouse?

**[00:50:58.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000924)  
How can the Philippines tout DHL as a principled partner for inclusive,

**[00:51:03.359] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000925)  
and social development?

**[00:51:05.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000926)  
How can a union buster genuinely claim that it works for the interests of ordinary people,

**[00:51:10.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000927)  
those that the MSMEs cater to and represent?

**[00:51:13.799] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000928)  
In line with this, we would like to call the attention of the DTI Secretary on this matter

**[00:51:18.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000929)  
and urgently review its partnership with DHL Philippines in light of the current

**[00:51:22.799] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000930)  
problems faced by its workers. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:51:25.559] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000932)  
Thank you, Cong. Perci.

**[00:51:26.640] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000933)  
That will be your last question.

**[00:51:27.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000934)  
Very valid.

**[00:51:28.520] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000935)  
Who may answer po?

**[00:51:29.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000936)  
Thank you, Madam Chair and Honorable Congressman.

**[00:51:32.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000939)  
Actually, the Supply Chain and Logistics Department in DTI is just to actually share with our

**[00:51:42.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000940)  
businesses how they can use the logistics companies to move their products.

**[00:51:48.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000941)  
Let's say, exporters ako.

**[00:51:50.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000942)  
So I now can engage or can meet with all of these logistics companies to explore the different

**[00:51:56.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000943)  
costs that they can incur or that they will incur.

**[00:52:02.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000944)  
When they move their products, kasi gusto natin ma-move yung mga products at the least possible

**[00:52:07.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000945)  
time and the least cost.

**[00:52:10.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000946)  
So iyon po ang engagement ng DTI with DHL.

**[00:52:15.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000947)  
But with your concerns po, we can work with the DOLE on this.

**[00:52:20.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000948)  
Ang importante na is to move the products, especially during calamity when we need to

**[00:52:25.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000949)  
move the food products to the different regions, especially that we are 7,600 islands,

**[00:52:31.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000950)  
and for the exporters

**[00:52:33.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000951)  
to engage

**[00:52:34.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000952)  
with all of these logistics companies so

**[00:52:37.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000953)  
they can see kung anong

**[00:52:38.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000954)  
mas beneficial

**[00:52:39.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000955)  
para sa kanila.

**[00:52:40.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000956)  
Yun po ang role

**[00:52:42.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000957)  
ng Supply Chain and Logistics Department namin sa DTI and the role of the logistics company once it comes

**[00:52:49.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000958)  
to the DTI. Doon po, to

**[00:52:52.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000959)  
encourage businesses po, to encourage rin, and to really make sure that the movements of the goods are

**[00:52:58.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000960)  
fast, cheap, and para ma-exhaust

**[00:53:02.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000961)  
natin yung full potential

**[00:53:03.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000962)  
ng mga logistics companies na ito in terms of movements of the products po.

**[00:53:10.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000963)  
Thank you, Madam Secretary. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:53:12.799] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000964)  
Paalala lamang po, i-review po natin yung agreements natin with this union buster, that is DHL. Marami pong salamat.

**[00:53:19.200] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000965)  
Thank you, Cong. Perci. And again, may we remind the DTI to submit the documents requested

**[00:53:24.119] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000966)  
so that the concerns of Representative Cendaña will be addressed.

**[00:53:27.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-000967)  
Next in line to ask from the majority is Representative Ando Oaminal.

**[00:53:36.520] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000968)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Good morning, Secretary, and to the members of the DTI family, and to our distinguished Chair.

**[00:53:49.559] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000969)  
Madam Chair, this representation received some reports on alleged irregularities

**[00:53:59.319] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000970)  
and alleged unlawful practices being observed by the department, particularly in the procurement division.

**[00:54:10.680] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000971)  
And so I wrote a letter to the good secretary as part of this representation's oversight functions.

**[00:54:19.079] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000972)  
And when I received and reviewed the documents forwarded to my office,

**[00:54:27.440] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000973)  
it seems that...

**[00:54:28.520] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000974)  
some of the claims are valid.

**[00:54:31.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000975)  
So in particular, I wrote about the procurement of several vehicles.

**[00:54:39.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000976)  
And I have several documents here, but I would like to focus first on the procurement with bidding number 24.057.

**[00:54:53.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000977)  
May staffa sa copy kung sino po yung sasagot.

**[00:54:56.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000978)  
Yung BAC chairperson po ay si...

**[00:55:01.720] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000979)  
Mr. Christian Ablan, and the HoPE is a certain attorney, Asec. Uvero.

**[00:55:10.359] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000980)  
However, sa NTP, iba po yung nag-sign, hindi po yung HoPE.

**[00:55:15.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000981)  
Sa contract agreement, iba po din yung nag-sign.

**[00:55:19.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000982)  
Si... ang BAC chairman ang nag-sign.

**[00:55:22.760] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000983)  
So, may we know under what authorities, bakit nagkakaiba-iba po yung HoPE for each transaction?

**[00:55:34.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-000984)  
Honorable Chair, I'll defer to Chris Ablan so he can respond to your queries and issues.

**[00:55:44.480] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000985)  
Madam Chair, thank you very much.

**[00:55:47.640] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000986)  
Mr. Chair, that is correct.

**[00:55:49.319] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000987)  
I am currently the BAC chair of the DTI Bids and Awards Committee.

**[00:55:54.480] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000988)  
We will look into the PR of this.

**[00:55:59.319] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000989)  
But as a matter of practice, and following the rules, the BAC chair does not sign a notice to proceed.

**[00:56:06.319] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000990)  
It's the HoPE.

**[00:56:10.680] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000991)  
And in the case of then-HoPE Asec. Uvero, there may have been a transfer since Asec. Uvero transferred to the Bureau of Customs.

**[00:56:27.920] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000992)  
We had a new HoPE.

**[00:56:30.839] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000993)  
And so, we will look into this PR and submit to the committee clarifications on what was mentioned by the good congressman.

**[00:56:41.119] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000994)  
Usec, andiyan naman po, finorward sa inyo yung copy ng documents that came from your office.

**[00:56:47.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000995)  
So, can you please check the contract agreement?

**[00:56:49.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000996)  
Isn't it your signature on that document?

**[00:56:59.079] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000997)  
Mr. Chair, for PR number...

**[00:57:02.520] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000998)  
For this particular PR, the Bids and Awards Committee head was not yet this person, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:11.160] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-000999)  
It was still Usec Boy Vismonte.

**[00:57:15.079] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001000)  
You were the one...

**[00:57:17.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001001)  
But you were the one who signed the BAC resolution for this transaction.

**[00:57:23.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001002)  
You signed the BAC resolution and you signed the contract agreement.

**[00:57:26.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001003)  
You signed the BAC resolution as the BAC chairperson, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:30.480] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001004)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:57:31.079] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001005)  
The document given to me by your staff is dated 2023.

**[00:57:34.359] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001006)  
I wasn't yet with the DTI.

**[00:57:37.160] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001007)  
So, we'll just check.

**[00:57:38.359] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001008)  
No, no, no.

**[00:57:38.799] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001009)  
Nandiyan po yung document, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:50.480] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001010)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:51.280] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001011)  
For this particular...

**[00:57:53.240] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001012)  
I just confirmed for BAC Resolution 280, series of 2024, procurement of vehicles from Toyota Pasong Tamo.

**[00:58:07.400] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001013)  
I signed as the chair of the Bids and Awards Committee.

**[00:58:09.920] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001014)  
That is correct, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:13.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001015)  
What document are you talking about?

**[00:58:15.079] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001016)  
You're talking about the BAC resolution, Mr. Chair, right?

**[00:58:18.240] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001017)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:19.200] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001018)  
I'm talking about BAC Resolution 280, series of 2024.

**[00:58:25.119] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001019)  
I signed as the chair of the BAC.

**[00:58:27.720] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001020)  
And then approved by our head of procuring entity, then Asec. Uvero.

**[00:58:34.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001021)  
And, Mr. Chair, did you also sign the contract agreement for that particular transaction?

**[00:58:56.400] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001022)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:57.599] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001023)  
I am signed here.

**[00:58:59.559] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001042)  
So, Mr. Chair, matanong ko lang po, bakit hindi po si HoPE ang nag-sign, and bakit po yung BAC chairperson ang nag-sign ng contract agreement?

**[00:59:10.319] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001043)  
We'll look into this, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:11.640] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001044)  
I think this was the time when there was a transition between Asec. Uvero and the new HoPE.

**[00:59:19.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001045)  
Mr. Chair, Section 11.2.5 of the 2016 Revised IRR of RA 9184 provides

**[00:59:28.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001046)  
that in no case shall the HoPE and/or the approving authority be the chairperson

**[00:59:34.480] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001047)  
or a member of the BAC.

**[00:59:36.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001048)  
Do you agree with this, Mr. Chair?

**[00:59:42.839] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001049)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:43.680] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001050)  
The congressman is correct.

**[00:59:45.400] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001051)  
We'll look into this February 5, 2025 contract agreement and align it with our records of

**[00:59:53.119] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001052)  
who was the head of procuring entity.

**[00:59:55.359] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001053)  
We'll clarify the committee, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:56.680] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001054)  
So, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:57.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001055)  
So, Mr. Chair, do you confirm that in this particular transaction, you violated Section 11.2.5 of RA 9184?

**[01:00:08.880] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001056)  
Mr. Chair, I don't recall signing this particular document, but the signature shows my signature.

**[01:00:15.200] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001057)  
So I'll verify with my records, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:17.559] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001058)  
What are you trying to imply, Mr. Chair, that you don't recall signing that document?

**[01:00:21.119] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001059)  
That document came from the, no less than the Office of the Secretary.

**[01:00:25.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001060)  
Are you denying now that that document is a certified copy of the original?

**[01:00:33.799] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001061)  
Mr. Chair, I would have to check my records.

**[01:00:37.440] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001062)  
I do have photocopies of all the contracts and resolutions I signed, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:44.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001063)  
Okay.

**[01:00:44.359] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001064)  
Mr. Chair, granting that this is a real document, kasi galing po sa inyo,

**[01:00:51.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001065)  
do you consider this a violation of Section

**[01:00:56.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001066)  
11.2.5 of the 2016 Revised IRR of RA 9184,

**[01:01:01.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001067)  
granting that this is a real document?

**[01:01:04.039] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001068)  
Kasi you are now questioning the validity of the document, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:12.720] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001069)  
I have to check with my records, Mr. Chair, as to this document.

**[01:01:16.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001070)  
Mr. Chair, my question is, granting that this is a real document.

**[01:01:33.680] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001071)  
Cong. Oaminal, while the Asec. is checking his documents, do you have other questions

**[01:01:39.119] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001072)  
regarding the budget proposal?

**[01:01:40.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001073)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:41.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001074)  
I have other documents here, different procurement documents that have the same issues

**[01:01:48.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001075)  
involving different officers of the agency.

**[01:01:52.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001076)  
Yung sa akin lang po kasi, of course, you are presenting your budget proposal

**[01:01:58.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001077)  
and humihingi po kayo ng budget.

**[01:02:01.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001078)  
It is our responsibility also to ensure that in the disbursement,

**[01:02:09.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001079)  
the funds that have been approved for the agency, regular and lawful po yung pag-disburse.

**[01:02:18.720] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001080)  
Eto ngayon, may nakikita po tayo na alleged irregularities.

**[01:02:23.680] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001081)  
So, how can we be assured, Mr. Chair, na hindi po ganito yung paggastos nila ng pera, Mr. Chair?

**[01:02:36.760] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001083)  
And my

**[01:02:38.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001084)  
question is very simple.

**[01:02:41.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001085)  
If this is a real document, which, kung peke ito, eh di you forwarded fake documents to my office.

**[01:02:51.000] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001086)  
All right, Cong. Oaminal, kanina kasi yung timer na-stuck.

**[01:02:55.200] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001087)  
I think that would be the last question.

**[01:02:57.480] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001088)  
But I want you to raise this concern.

**[01:03:01.200] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001089)  
If you can do a sidebar after this, kasi I think this is a very valid concern, Cong. Oaminal.

**[01:03:08.119] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001090)  
But at the very least, can we hear, Mr. Chair,

**[01:03:10.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001091)  
the response of the agency if they affirm and confirm that there is indeed a violation

**[01:03:17.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001092)  
of our procurement law, Mr. Chair?

**[01:03:19.319] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001093)  
Okay.

**[01:03:19.559] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001094)  
For the record, last response for our representative.

**[01:03:24.319] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001095)  
Thank you.

**[01:03:24.640] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001096)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:03:28.000] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001097)  
I signed as the head of our admin service.

**[01:03:34.359] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001098)  
So, we will have to check whether the hat I was wearing in signing this contract agreement

**[01:03:39.520] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001099)  
is as the head of our HR and admin service.

**[01:03:43.760] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001100)  
By our rules, I am authorized to sign some contracts, particularly contracts of lease.

**[01:03:49.520] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001101)  
So, we have to get back to the good congressman if whether I signed the contract agreement

**[01:03:53.760] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001102)  
as being the head of our admin service or being the HoPE.

**[01:03:57.880] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001103)  
If you look at the contract agreement, it does not say that I am the HoPE.

**[01:04:00.720] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001104)  
It does say that I am assistant secretary for our management services group.

**[01:04:04.200] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001105)  
So, we will clarify this matter, Madam Chair, with the good congressman.

**[01:04:07.920] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001106)  
Uh, but I assure you that there's

**[01:04:09.240] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001107)  
no irregularities.

**[01:04:10.799] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001108)  
I do sign contracts on a different hat.

**[01:04:15.400] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001109)  
In this particular case, I do think I was signing it as the head of our admin service,

**[01:04:20.279] DTI BAC Chair Christian Ablan** (asr-001110)  
Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:20.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001111)  
Thank you, Asec.

**[01:04:21.680] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001112)  
Thank you po.

**[01:04:24.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001113)  
I will be interpellating again sa second round.

**[01:04:27.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001114)  
So, baka po pagdating sa second round, meron nang sagot and ma-clarify na

**[01:04:32.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001115)  
that the documents are indeed not fake.

**[01:04:34.839] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001116)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[01:04:35.960] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001117)  
Exactly. Thank you so much.

**[01:04:37.640] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001118)  
Next on the, um,

**[01:04:39.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-001119)  
list would be from the minority Representative Iris Montes.

**[01:04:43.559] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001120)  
Thank you.

**[01:04:45.599] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001121)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:04:47.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001122)  
Magandang umaga po sa lahat.

**[01:04:51.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001123)  
The Philippines continues to run on a trade deficit.

**[01:04:54.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001124)  
Ano po, ah, ano po ang specific allocations natin in the 2027 budget of the DTI

**[01:05:01.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001125)  
that directly addresses or nakatuon po sa pag-reduce ng ating trade deficit?

**[01:05:06.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001126)  
While I understand sa presentation po ninyo,

**[01:05:10.559] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001127)  
ni-report ninyo na nag-grow yung exports, pero pag ibinangga pa rin po natin, may trade deficit pa rin po tayo.

**[01:05:17.319] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001128)  
Halimbawa po, ano ang puwede nating magawa para sa 2027 budget po ninyo na mapalakas natin yung export diversification natin,

**[01:05:26.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001129)  
yung mga import substitution or industry modernization?

**[01:05:30.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001130)  
Thank you po.

**[01:05:31.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001131)  
Ah, thank you, Madam Chair and Honorable Congresswoman.

**[01:05:35.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001132)  
We can really strengthen dito sa exports, no, kasi

**[01:05:38.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001133)  
we've seen, um, nung 2025, all-time high, tapos from January to June, pataas din po.

**[01:05:45.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001134)  
So, this is where we see that there's really potential in really actually pushing the Philippine product.

**[01:05:51.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001135)  
So, that's one.

**[01:05:52.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001136)  
Number two is providing market access also for local products to be sold within the country

**[01:05:58.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001137)  
kasi malaki rin po ang potential ng local product, and the market is so big in the Philippines.

**[01:06:04.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001138)  
Actually, that is what attracts also the foreign investors in our country.

**[01:06:08.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001139)  
So doon po naka-concentrate now, um, naka-concentrate ang DTI, and also our foreign trade attachés in the 21 countries globally.

**[01:06:20.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001140)  
They hold the opportunities and possibilities, but we still need, of course, to join more

**[01:06:26.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001141)  
trade shows kasi kailangan natin i-showcase din yung ating mga produkto.

**[01:06:30.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001142)  
There are some trade shows that we've actually joined na actually nagbaka-sakali.

**[01:06:38.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001143)  
Very ambitious sila,

**[01:06:39.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001144)  
but we've seen a lot of growth here.

**[01:06:42.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001146)  
Like, let's just example lang,

**[01:06:44.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001147)  
we joined the Hong Kong Cosmoprof.

**[01:06:46.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001148)  
Yung kalaban natin actually is very strong, which is the Chinese products, the Korean

**[01:06:55.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001149)  
products, and so many other products.

**[01:06:57.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001150)  
What we did was

**[01:06:58.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001151)  
a strategic focus on our coconut products, or coconut-based products.

**[01:07:06.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001152)  
Doon tayo nag-concentrate.

**[01:07:08.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001153)  
So we had an investment of 8 million, and we generated 150 million.

**[01:07:13.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001154)  
So it goes to show, kung magiging strategic lang tayo sa ating pagsali rin sa mga different

**[01:07:20.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001155)  
trade shows, and to join more trade shows globally, we can really increase our exports

**[01:07:27.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001156)  
even more.

**[01:07:29.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001157)  
With the free trade agreement, which I've also mentioned earlier, the 23 that the Marcos administration

**[01:07:39.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001158)  
is concluding,

**[01:07:40.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001159)  
yung iba, we have five more to go,

**[01:07:42.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001160)  
so magiging 23 siya.

**[01:07:43.839] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001161)  
That will really open a lot of the big markets to our products, kasi ang nangyayari, we have

**[01:07:50.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001162)  
the products, but when we export it, medyo mataas because of the tariff.

**[01:07:55.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001163)  
But now that we have preferential tariffs, that will also give us more opportunities

**[01:07:59.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001164)  
to sell.

**[01:08:00.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001165)  
So doon tayo naka-rely sa exports.

**[01:08:03.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001166)  
Then expansion of investors in the Philippines.

**[01:08:06.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001167)  
So we keep on.

**[01:08:08.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001168)  
Upgrading also our incentive schemes.

**[01:08:11.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001169)  
In fact, we have the SIPP 2026, no?

**[01:08:14.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001170)  
So we have up to 99 years na yung ownership – or lease ng land, I mean.

**[01:08:19.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001171)  
So we keep on upgrading based on the needs of the investors that come in.

**[01:08:24.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001172)  
I've been going to the presidential visits, and a lot of the companies that have already

**[01:08:30.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001173)  
businesses here continue to expand.

**[01:08:33.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001174)  
So doon tayo nakafocus din.

**[01:08:36.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001175)  
And of course, there's a Middle East crisis.

**[01:08:38.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001176)  
So medyo naano ng konti,

**[01:08:40.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001177)  
but yung exports natin

**[01:08:42.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001178)  
tuloy-tuloy ang

**[01:08:43.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001179)  
pag-increase pa rin.

**[01:08:44.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001180)  
And the strength of the local market,

**[01:08:46.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001181)  
the products that are being sold

**[01:08:48.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001182)  
also locally have also increased

**[01:08:51.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001183)  
compared to last year.

**[01:08:52.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001184)  
So naka-monitor po kami doon

**[01:08:55.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001185)  
sa talagang, ano, increase doon.

**[01:08:59.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001186)  
Doon kami naka-monitor.

**[01:09:00.759] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001187)  
Then for export,

**[01:09:02.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001188)  
we are joining more trade shows.

**[01:09:04.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001189)  
Libre po ito.

**[01:09:05.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001190)  
Lahat ng trade shows ng DTI,

**[01:09:07.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001191)  
globally and locally, are all free

**[01:09:09.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001192)  
for all the companies

**[01:09:11.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001193)  
that want to, want to join.

**[01:09:13.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001194)  
And then another focus now is the halal.

**[01:09:15.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001195)  
So ang naging glitch lang doon

**[01:09:20.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001196)  
is the certification,

**[01:09:21.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001197)  
but we have now also engaged

**[01:09:24.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001198)  
foreign companies

**[01:09:25.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001199)  
that will certify the local products.

**[01:09:28.839] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001200)  
We have the product, but sometimes we don't have the certification.

**[01:09:31.839] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001201)  
So doon din po kami nakaano.

**[01:09:35.199] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001202)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:09:37.239] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001203)  
One way of to address it, as you said po, is increasing exports.

**[01:09:42.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001204)  
And the other is lowering importation.

**[01:09:45.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001205)  
So in terms of our, we are so, alam naman po natin yung dependent sa importation.

**[01:09:50.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001206)  
In terms naman po of

**[01:09:51.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001207)  
developing the capacity of our local industry so that we are not heavily reliant on importation.

**[01:09:56.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001208)  
Ano po nakapaloob sa 2027 budget that will help our local industry compete?

**[01:10:03.239] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001209)  
And para hindi na po, baka puwede nating i-produce dito, do you have convergence with appropriate government agencies

**[01:10:10.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001210)  
so that ma-strengthen natin yung interagency cooperation at mas mapalawak po natin

**[01:10:15.719] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001211)  
at magamit ng mas maayos po yung pondo natin?

**[01:10:20.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001212)  
Tama po iyan.

**[01:10:21.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001213)  
Honorable Congresswoman, no, we take a whole-government approach, meaning we work closely.

**[01:10:26.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001214)  
Because for DTI, ang focus namin is really income and trade.

**[01:10:30.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001215)  
So yung products binibigay sa amin, kami naghahanap ng market para maibenta itong mga produkto.

**[01:10:36.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001216)  
So we work closely with TESDA because doon they concentrate now on mga industries that need talent.

**[01:10:44.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001217)  
So like for the semiconductor industry, heavy din po yung training nila.

**[01:10:48.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001218)  
In fact, we have a project with...

**[01:10:51.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001219)  
ADB that we're just waiting to conclude, which is the Ascend,

**[01:10:56.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001220)  
which is actually training po ito for industries that are...

**[01:11:02.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001221)  
where we can see that there's really a lot of potential in the global space,

**[01:11:06.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001222)  
which is semiconductors, may aviation, there's also minerals, pharma, renewable energy.

**[01:11:16.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001223)  
So very strategic na po kami.

**[01:11:19.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001224)  
So wherever the strength is.

**[01:11:20.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001225)  
Doon po kami nakafocus.

**[01:11:22.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001226)  
So we work closely with DOST.

**[01:11:24.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001227)  
We work closely with TESDA for the training.

**[01:11:27.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001228)  
And then once they're trained there, sa amin po naman po yung nag, ah, pagbenta.

**[01:11:33.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001229)  
So the training is not in DTI.

**[01:11:35.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001230)  
In DTI, nandito yung finding, um, opportunities for them to sell their products, whether locally or globally.

**[01:11:44.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001231)  
Okay.

**[01:11:44.639] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001232)  
Thank you so much.

**[01:11:45.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001233)  
Thank you.

**[01:11:46.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001234)  
Moving on to my next question po, tungkol naman po sa SB Corporation performance.

**[01:11:50.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001235)  
Can you please give us an update of the, um, ang latest performance update po ng SB Corp,

**[01:11:57.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001236)  
loan releases, yung mga repayment rates? Meron kayo yung reach ninyo sa MSMEs?

**[01:12:04.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001237)  
Okay.

**[01:12:04.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001238)  
Thank you, Honorable Congresswoman.

**[01:12:06.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001239)  
For SB Corporation, aggressive po kami sa pag-promote.

**[01:12:10.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001240)  
And then what we did now was, in the past, SB Corporation was a financial institution that just has one big loan.

**[01:12:18.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001241)  
So some people, they cannot relate

**[01:12:21.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001242)  
to one big loan.

**[01:12:23.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001243)  
Mas, um, mas naging madali para sa kanila or mas naging, um, specific.

**[01:12:31.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001244)  
It's easier for us to loan out the money kung specific because then they can relate.

**[01:12:35.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001245)  
Ay, para sa akin to.

**[01:12:36.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001246)  
Ay, may loan pala para sa akin.

**[01:12:38.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001247)  
Ay, pwede pala ako dito.

**[01:12:39.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001248)  
So naging aggressive po kami in promoting this loans program.

**[01:12:44.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001249)  
Especially, naging, um, attractive din po ito because, um, up to 5 million, no collateral.

**[01:12:50.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001250)  
And then one year rin po, no payment of principal and interest.

**[01:12:55.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001251)  
So this is for 3-year loan, 5-years loan.

**[01:13:00.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001252)  
So ang dami na po nag-avail, no.

**[01:13:02.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001253)  
And then also the time to get the loan, it used to be months.

**[01:13:08.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001254)  
But now it's 7 to 10 days.

**[01:13:11.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001255)  
But now we've got a backlog because madami na pong umuutang.

**[01:13:15.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001256)  
And now we need more funds to accommodate

**[01:13:19.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001257)  
more, um,

**[01:13:20.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001258)  
more MSMEs that can loan,

**[01:13:22.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001259)  
Especially because of the Middle East crisis.

**[01:13:24.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001260)  
That's why nandito po kami to—

**[01:13:26.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001261)  
those are the loan amounts that we need sana

**[01:13:33.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001262)  
makausa din yung mga negosyo.

**[01:13:36.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001263)  
So for the SB Corporation also, we also streamlined.

**[01:13:41.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001264)  
We also made the requirements very flexible, very easy, and very strategic.

**[01:13:49.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001265)  
Like for MSMEs.

**[01:13:52.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001266)  
Up to 5 million, they need only the business name registration.

**[01:13:55.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001267)  
Kasi kailangan sila maging government compliant.

**[01:13:58.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001268)  
Then they need the mayor's permit or business permit.

**[01:14:01.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001269)  
Then the bank...

**[01:14:02.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001270)  
Yung bank details lang po.

**[01:14:04.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001271)  
And then also the picture of their company.

**[01:14:06.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001272)  
And puwede na po silang umutang.

**[01:14:08.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001273)  
So we have for Sari-Sari Store.

**[01:14:10.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001274)  
We have for OFW.

**[01:14:12.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001275)  
We have for Women Enterprise Fund to encourage double income in a family.

**[01:14:16.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001276)  
We have also MSME.

**[01:14:19.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001277)  
Then we also have...

**[01:14:20.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001278)  
MSME Fund.

**[01:14:21.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001279)  
We also have the e-transport.

**[01:14:23.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001280)  
So those who want to shift from...

**[01:14:25.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001281)  
Let's say yung mga riders who, let's say, Grab drivers or Lalamove.

**[01:14:31.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001282)  
They have a motorcycle.

**[01:14:32.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001283)  
They want to shift to EV or tricycle drivers.

**[01:40:08.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002013)

**[01:40:17.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002015)

**[01:40:22.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002016)

**[01:40:24.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002017)  
Of how come or for them to encourage them to invest here in the Philippines.

**[01:40:31.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002018)  
Yes, Madam Chair. Dahil po, in the midst of this transport crisis,

**[01:40:35.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002019)

**[01:40:39.639] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002020)

**[01:40:49.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002021)  
So hindi po naririsolve yung transport crisis at this moment.

**[01:40:53.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002022)  
I'd like to touch a bit.

**[01:40:54.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002023)  
on yung EVIS. Dahil nabasa rin po natin yung executive order.

**[01:40:59.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002024)  
And we'd like to flag na it is similar doon sa language ng race program.

**[01:41:07.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002025)  
In which na hindi rin po makakaya sa tingin natin ng mga lokal na ma-meet yung kanyang malalaking mga requirements.

**[01:41:17.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002026)  
Case in point, manufacturers must commit at least 5 billion pesos in capital.

**[01:41:21.679] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002027)  
Can I ask

**[01:41:22.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002028)  
Kung sa tingin ba natin kaya ma-meet ng ating local manufacturers yung ganitong pangailangan in capital?

**[01:41:36.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002029)  
I defer to Usec. Perry regarding the details of the EVIS.

**[01:41:40.119] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002030)  
Yes, please, Yusec.

**[01:41:41.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002031)  
Thank you, Madam Secretary, Madam Chair, and Representative Co.

**[01:41:45.599] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002032)  
Yes po, meron tayong minimum capitalization requirement.

**[01:41:48.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002033)  
But normally ang mangyayari dyan, like for example, if we're talking about Francisco Motors,

**[01:41:54.239] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002034)  
meron din mo silang foreign partner that they are in discussion with now.

**[01:41:59.360] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002035)  
For the EVIS.

**[01:42:01.000] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002036)  
So I'd like to assure you po that we are working closely also with the local manufacturers including si Francisco.

**[01:42:07.639] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002037)  
Nasa akin na po yung data na tinatanong nyo kanina kung sino-sino yung mga local companies under the CARS program.

**[01:42:14.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002038)  
Because yung mga parts manufacturer po, may mga Filipino companies doon.

**[01:42:18.840] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002039)  
For example, Manly Plastics, Valerie Products, F-Tech Philippines, SMRC Automotive Technology, Roberts Automotive, and Tri-R Allied Industries.

**[01:42:29.800] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-002040)  
So mga Filipino companies po ito that form part of the supply chain of the two registered model participants in the CARS program.

**[01:42:38.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002041)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:42:40.119] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002042)  
Pero again, parang we'd like to distinguish yung doon sa gumagawa ng parts for foreign automotive companies.

**[01:42:48.239] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002043)  
And to clarify, yung mga namensyon po ba, are they providing parts for foreign automotive companies?

**[01:42:54.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002044)  
Yun pong namang nabanggit natin, providing parts to the model participants.

**[01:43:01.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002045)  
And ang gusto din natin ay, paano bang nasusuportahan yung ating local automotive companies?

**[01:43:06.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002046)  
So for now po, ang pinaflag, dahil po sa high capitalization requirement, sabihin man natin may foreign partners sila,

**[01:43:13.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002047)  
paano po ba masisigurado na ito na masusuportahan yung local manufacturers natin, Madam Chair?

**[01:43:22.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002048)  
Actually, we will continue to work with them.

**[01:43:26.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002049)  
Kasi sometimes, let's say, a foreign company opens, then may partner sila na local, kasi usually 60-40 yan.

**[01:43:32.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002050)  
So part of their requirement is to buy from local companies.

**[01:43:38.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002051)  
So for now, yung mga ibang local companies, supply them.

**[01:43:44.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002052)  
So kahit pa paano, it's already a sure buy, rather than for them to establish something so big.

**[01:43:53.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002053)  
Unlike, example, mag-umpisa sila ng maliit, and then later they grow as that foreign company grows.

**[01:43:59.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002054)  
But ang importante is, meron silang 60%.

**[01:44:03.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002055)  
Or they are part owner of a bigger pie.

**[01:44:06.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002056)  
And then we also, Madam Chair and Honorable Congresswoman, we have started already an industry consultation.

**[01:44:15.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002057)  
Actually, we start tomorrow.

**[01:44:17.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002058)  
So one industry is the AI and digital industry.

**[01:44:23.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002059)  
We want to know exactly what they need from the department, how the department can help them as they grow,

**[01:44:31.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002060)  
and how DEFA can.

**[01:44:32.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002061)  
Also help them, help their businesses or help the industry.

**[01:44:39.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002062)  
And also maging advantage sa kanila yung digital economic framework agreement na ito.

**[01:44:46.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002063)  
And then we will also talk to the consolidators, because sometimes may mga produkto, let's say may local products, ang problem is they don't know how...

**[01:44:55.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002064)  
to export.

**[01:44:57.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002065)  
They also don't know how to do the requirements abroad.

**[01:44:59.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002066)  
But through the consolidators that have already existing markets globally,

**[01:45:05.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002067)  
madali na lang sila makapasok.

**[01:45:06.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002068)  
So we are using consolidators to promote also the products.

**[01:45:11.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002069)  
And then also the food industry where we see a lot of strength also in our exports.

**[01:45:18.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002070)  
Not just exports is one, but there's also a huge local market which we need to also address.

**[01:45:27.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002071)  
And then also wearables.

**[01:45:30.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002072)  
So those are the, at least for this week, ito yung mga kinakausap namin.

**[01:45:35.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002073)

**[01:45:39.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002074)

**[01:45:43.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002075)

**[01:45:47.239] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002077)

**[01:45:47.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002078)  
po sa update sa schedule.

**[01:45:49.639] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002079)

**[01:45:55.639] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002080)

**[01:46:04.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002083)

**[01:46:12.199] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002084)

**[01:46:16.159] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002085)

**[01:46:25.719] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002087)

**[01:46:37.679] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002089)

**[01:46:47.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002091)
