**[01:51:00.119] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002164)  
Isa sa mga pangunahing concerns ng ating mga consumers ay

**[01:51:03.639] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002165)  
ang pagsilit ng presyo ng mga bilihin

**[01:51:06.239] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002166)  
at bilang chair ng Price Coordinating Council,

**[01:51:08.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002167)  
maaari ba nating malaman kung ano ang mga hakbang

**[01:51:11.719] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002168)  
na ginawa ng DTI para

**[01:51:13.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002169)  
ma-stabilize ang prices ng basic necessities

**[01:51:16.719] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002170)  
at prime commodities,

**[01:51:18.639] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002171)  
lalong-lalo na sa panahon ng sunod-sunod na kalamidad?

**[01:51:22.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002172)  
Thank you, Honorable Congresswoman and Honorable Chair.

**[01:51:28.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002173)  
Last year, we did not have any price increase

**[01:51:30.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002174)  
for basic necessities and prime commodities,

**[01:51:33.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002175)  
the list that's controlled by the DTI.

**[01:51:35.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002176)  
We keep a very close relationship and coordination

**[01:51:38.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002177)  
with the manufacturers and also the retailers.

**[01:51:44.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002178)  
We do our best to really make sure na hindi gumagalaw yung prices.

**[01:51:49.079] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002179)  
In fact, in spite of the Middle East crisis,

**[01:51:53.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002180)  
from January

**[01:51:55.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002181)  
or from last year until May,

**[01:51:57.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002182)  
we did not have any price increase

**[01:51:59.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002183)  
for the basic necessities and prime commodities,

**[01:52:01.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002184)  
dahil maganda yung relationship ng DTI

**[01:52:05.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002185)  
at saka yung mga manufacturers whose products

**[01:52:08.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002186)  
are part of the basic necessities and prime commodities.

**[01:52:11.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002187)  
So again, we continue to work closely with them.

**[01:52:16.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002188)  
We keep a win-win relationship with them.

**[01:52:20.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002189)  
So kung may mga needs sila sa ibang mga departments

**[01:52:23.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002190)  
to keep their operations going,

**[01:52:27.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002191)  
to keep them also kaipapano,

**[01:52:30.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002192)  
to find other markets for them globally,

**[01:52:33.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002193)  
we do that for them para at least mabigay din nila sa taong bayan

**[01:52:40.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002194)  
yung mababang presyo ng or yung stable na prices

**[01:52:43.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002195)  
ng basic necessities and prime commodities.

**[01:52:46.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002196)  
Nagmamonitor din kami nationwide.

**[01:52:49.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002197)  
We have 18 regional offices

**[01:52:51.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002198)  
and we do this on a daily basis.

**[01:52:53.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002199)  
May departamento talaga in each of the regional offices for this.

**[01:52:58.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002200)  
So we only monitor the supermarkets and the groceries.

**[01:53:04.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002201)  
So we have not experienced any...

**[01:53:10.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002202)  
Yung mga hindi sumusunod, halos lahat sila sumusunod.

**[01:53:13.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002203)  
And if hindi sila sumusunod, sinasabihan namin sila.

**[01:53:16.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002204)  
But in general,

**[01:53:20.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002205)  
or in majority,

**[01:53:22.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002206)  
they really follow the rules of the DTI.

**[01:53:28.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002207)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:53:29.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002208)  
Ang reports sa atin ay halos lahat sumusunod.

**[01:53:32.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002209)  
Yung mga hindi sumusunod,

**[01:53:35.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002210)  
anong naging parusa sa kanila?

**[01:53:36.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002211)  
Meron ba tayong mga napanagot sa mga hindi sumusunod?

**[01:53:41.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002212)  
Actually, we have action or we enforce.

**[01:53:47.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002213)  
We monitor and we enforce

**[01:53:48.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002214)  
for those that don't follow.

**[01:53:51.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002215)  
I'll forward you, ma'am, to Gino

**[01:53:57.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002216)  
to report what we do with those that don't follow.

**[01:54:01.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002217)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:54:02.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002218)  
Gusto po natin malaman yung extent ng price monitoring activities.

**[01:54:07.599] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002219)  
Nabanggit na ang sakop lang yung mga retailers, grocery supermarkets natin.

**[01:54:12.119] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002220)  
Pero tama ko ba hindi dito kasama yung mga public markets?

**[01:54:16.719] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002221)  
For the public markets,

**[01:54:17.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002222)  
we defer to the DA.

**[01:54:19.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002223)  
So the DA is the one in charge of the wet market.

**[01:54:25.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002224)  
Ang DTI, ang kinokontrol niya,

**[01:54:27.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002225)  
are the supermarkets and the groceries

**[01:54:29.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002226)  
for the basic necessities and prime commodities.

**[01:54:32.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002227)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:54:34.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002228)  
May we hear from Asek Malyari

**[01:54:36.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002229)  
on the report on the price monitoring?

**[01:54:39.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002230)  
Asek, please. Thank you.

**[01:54:42.639] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002231)  
Good afternoon, Madam Chair.

**[01:54:47.280] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002232)  
Um...

**[01:54:47.760] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002233)  
As reported by our dear Secretary,

**[01:54:50.079] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002234)  
we have very good compliance po na sa retail networks natin

**[01:54:53.239] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002235)  
ng supermarkets and groceries.

**[01:54:54.719] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002236)  
And these are the ones prioritized by our price monitors,

**[01:54:57.760] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002237)  
yung mga malalapit po at malalaki na chains.

**[01:55:01.239] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002238)  
Because, again, with our relationship with them,

**[01:55:06.199] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002239)  
single pricing sila usually

**[01:55:09.199] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002240)  
based po dun sa suggested retail price bulletin natin.

**[01:55:12.560] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002241)  
So nasusunod po siya automatically throughout the retail network, no?

**[01:55:17.280] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002242)  
And if and when meron tayong nakikita na slightly above

**[01:55:22.119] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002243)  
the suggested retail prices,

**[01:55:23.560] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002244)  
we issue them letter of inquiry muna.

**[01:55:26.840] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002245)  
Letter of inquiry and then the establishment

**[01:55:29.239] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002246)  
nakakapagpaliwanag po sila kung bakit slightly mataas

**[01:55:33.199] DTI price-monitoring and product-standards official** (asr-002247)  
yung kanilang presyuhan sa ating SRP.

**[01:55:38.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002248)  
Madam Chair, meron ba tayong...

**[01:55:40.679] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002249)  
Pwedeng masumitin na lang nun ang DTI,

**[01:55:42.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002250)  
pero yung policy natin pagdating dito sa PEDA,

**[01:55:47.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002251)  
dito sa penalties, sa mga fines,

**[01:55:49.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002252)  
ano yung mga levels ng accountability

**[01:55:53.079] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002253)  
para matiyak din yung transparency nila.

**[01:55:55.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002254)  
At syempre, hindi naman natin yung kanilang paliwanag,

**[01:55:58.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002255)  
kailangan din natin matiyak na walang abuso

**[01:56:02.199] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002256)  
pagdating sa pagpepresyo. Ayan.

**[01:56:04.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002257)

**[02:14:27.320] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002698)

**[02:14:32.520] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002701)  
million

**[02:14:32.800] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002702)  
po.

**[02:14:33.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002703)  
Maybe you are,

**[02:14:35.599] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002704)  
Madam Chair, you're referring to the

**[02:14:37.760] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002705)  
penalties paid by these

**[02:14:39.760] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002706)  
violators, is

**[02:14:41.360] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002707)  
that correct?

**[02:14:42.040] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002708)  
Uh, the retail estimated

**[02:14:44.040] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002709)  
retail amount of the values of

**[02:14:46.000] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002710)  
of the substandard steel products po.

**[02:14:48.559] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002711)  
Okay. So what are we doing to those violators? May kaso po iyan sa DTI.

**[02:14:53.880] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002712)  
How many ang finile natin na kaso against these violators?

**[02:14:58.840] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002713)  
We will submit the details of the cases, Your Honor.

**[02:15:02.000] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002714)  
Please submit to us. And in relation to this one, if you look at 7394, the penalty is light.

**[02:15:12.199] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002715)  
Yet, if you equate the sufferings that the Filipino people will incur because of the substandard products,

**[02:15:25.800] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002716)  
for me, it does not really commensurate.

**[02:15:30.719] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002717)  
Because 5,000 to 10,000 pesos or imprisonment of arresto mayor,

**[02:15:39.639] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002718)  
two months,

**[02:15:40.480] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002719)  
to prisión correccional, one year only.

**[02:15:45.000] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002720)  
Do you think this should be amended likewise to increase the penalty?

**[02:15:50.280] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002721)  
Thank you, Representative.

**[02:15:52.239] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002722)  
That would be your last question.

**[02:15:53.920] Rep. Mauricio G. Domogan** (asr-002723)  
That's my last question, Madam Chair.

**[02:15:57.760] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002725)  
Again, I appreciate your support, Your Honor.

**[02:16:00.520] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002726)  
In fact, we have a strengthening bill that we are recommending.

**[02:16:05.079] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002727)  
One is the visitorial powers of the DTI for us to go into these facilities.

**[02:16:10.520] DTI assistant secretary addressed as Gino (functional attribution)** (asr-002728)  
Second, it's for the authority of the Department Secretary to increase the penalties as necessary.

**[02:16:17.719] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002729)  
Thank you, Asec.

**[02:16:27.520] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002730)  
For the appreciation of this committee, may we also ask for the basis of that amount at

**[02:16:34.359] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002731)  
saan pong specific line item in the budget?

**[02:16:37.800] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002732)  
Thank you, Representative Domogan.

**[02:16:39.120] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-002734)  
And now for the part of the minority, the next one to ask is Representative Roberto Nazal.

**[02:16:46.519] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002738)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:16:48.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002739)  
Good afternoon, Madam Secretary, and to the DTI family.

**[02:16:53.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002742)  
Madam Chair, Madam Secretary, the DTI OSEC budget is being reduced from 7.392 billion

**[02:17:02.679] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002743)  
in 2026 to 6.744 billion in the 2027 NEP.

**[02:17:09.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002744)  
But within the smaller budget, MSME development increases, while several projects

**[02:17:15.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002746)  
and productive expenditures are reduced.

**[02:17:19.599] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002747)  
So before we go into individual programs, what is the DTI's principal economic strategy

**[02:17:25.679] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002748)  
for 2027?

**[02:17:27.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002749)  
What measurable result should Congress expect from this 6.7 billion budget in additional

**[02:17:36.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002750)  
businesses, jobs, investments, and exports?

**[02:17:44.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002751)  
Thank you, Honorable Congressman and Madam Chair.

**[02:17:47.559] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002752)  
Actually three main focus talagang DTI.

**[02:17:50.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002753)  
Number one is to push for foreign trade and international investments, including expansion

**[02:17:56.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002754)  
of existing investors that are here in the Philippines.

**[02:17:59.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002755)  
But we also need additional funds to promote the Philippines, to promote our ecozone, to

**[02:18:05.679] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002756)  
promote our Create More Act, to promote the different incentive schemes that we have.

**[02:18:12.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002757)  
And to also promote our free trade agreements that President Marcos will go down in history

**[02:18:19.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002758)  
as the president with the most free trade agreement.

**[02:18:21.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002759)  
Kasi importante ito para sa mga investors to know that if they manufacture their products

**[02:18:26.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002760)  
here and they export to important countries, they have preferential tariff.

**[02:18:33.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002761)  
So importante din po yun.

**[02:18:35.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002762)  
That's why we were requesting actually budgets for these three main mandates of DTI.

**[02:18:41.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002763)  
Number two.

**[02:18:42.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002764)  
Consumer protection and fair trade, which the Honorable Congressman mentioned that we

**[02:18:48.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002765)  
need to now be more vigilant or to really check more and to enforce and to monitor the

**[02:18:57.599] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002766)  
products that are being sold, manufactured in the Philippines to at least protect our

**[02:19:03.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002767)  
consumers from, I mean, from mishaps or from things, untoward events or untoward incidents.

**[02:19:11.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002775)  
And number three, the MSME development.

**[02:19:14.280] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002776)  
This is a sector that we cannot ignore.

**[02:19:16.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002777)  
Ninety-nine point six percent of the business establishment is MSME, sixty-two percent is

**[02:19:21.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002778)  
the labor force.

**[02:19:23.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002779)  
This is actually, if you look at it, this is a low hanging fruit.

**[02:19:28.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002780)  
kasi nandito na sila.

**[02:19:31.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002781)  
So if we support them, this will grow the economy quickly and this will also provide

**[02:19:36.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002782)  
jobs also quickly.

**[02:19:38.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002783)  
So tatlo lang po yung nakafocus kami for this MSME development.

**[02:19:42.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002786)  
for this year, which we feel will kasi for DTI, ang focus namin is really income

**[02:19:48.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002787)  
and trade.

**[02:19:49.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002788)  
So doon tayo talagang umiikot.

**[02:19:51.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002789)  
And the exports, which 2025, we've seen an all-time high, meaning from the beginning

**[02:19:57.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002790)  
of time, 15.4 percent increase.

**[02:20:00.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002791)  
And then from 2026, January up to June, again, increase in spite and despite of the Middle

**[02:20:06.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002792)  
East crisis.

**[02:20:07.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002793)  
In fact, sa June, we increased 24.1 percent.

**[02:20:10.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002794)  
So this also shows that the Philippine products are really ready for the global market, considering

**[02:20:17.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002795)  
hindi pa na-conclude and hindi pa nag-sign yung important free trade agreements na hinihintay

**[02:20:23.479] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002796)  
na lang po natin.

**[02:20:25.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002797)  
Madam Chair, for MSME, yan kasi ang malapit sa puso ko eh, for MSME development,

**[02:20:32.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002798)  
what are your main programs?

**[02:20:33.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002799)  
Okay.

**[02:20:34.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002800)  
The main programs of MSME, number one is market access, kasi products without the market

**[02:20:40.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002801)  
access.

**[02:20:41.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002802)  
Or without providing market or areas for them to sell will just remain as products.

**[02:20:46.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002803)  
That's number one.

**[02:20:48.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002804)  
Number two, they need finance.

**[02:20:50.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002805)  
We have the SB Corporation, but the funds is not enough anymore because of the Middle

**[02:20:56.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002806)  
East crisis.

**[02:20:57.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002807)  
And because of the extensive marketing that we've been doing, marami na po ang nakakaalam

**[02:21:02.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002808)  
na SB Corp is the financial institution where the MSMEs can loan na up to five million po,

**[02:21:11.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002809)  
no collateral, which everybody knows.

**[02:21:14.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002810)  
The small and medium enterprises don't have collateral.

**[02:21:17.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002811)  
And then one year, no payment of principal and interest.

**[02:21:21.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002812)  
So may palugit po sila na makausad ng negosyo nila.

**[02:21:25.479] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002813)  
Then they can start paying on the second year.

**[02:21:27.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002814)  
So we've introduced newer loan programs.

**[02:21:32.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002815)  
Aside from the P3?

**[02:21:33.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002816)  
Aside from—pardon?

**[02:21:35.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002817)  
Aside from yung P3?

**[02:21:37.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002818)  
Yung Pondo sa Pagbabago?

**[02:21:38.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002821)  
So we have loan programs that were very strategic and specific, especially for the Middle East

**[02:21:45.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002822)  
crisis.

**[02:21:46.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002823)  
And I don't know if you want to hear the loan programs again.

**[02:21:49.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002824)  
So the OFW Negosyo Funds, the Women Enterprise Fund, which we encourage now double income

**[02:21:55.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002825)  
in a family.

**[02:21:56.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002826)  
Kasi we know that pag double income, better education, better quality of life.

**[02:22:01.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002827)  
They can now – the tourism will also go up, then the indirect industries, and then

**[02:22:06.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002828)  
we also have – and our last was the Youth Enterprise Fund.

**[02:22:10.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002829)  
We try to encourage now the youth that are always on the social media to use their time

**[02:22:16.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002830)  
wisely and to start pagnenegosyo at an early age.

**[02:22:20.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002831)  
So in terms of market access, ano po yung programs natin?

**[02:22:25.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002832)  
Uh.

**[02:22:25.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002833)  
Specific programs?

**[02:22:26.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002834)  
We have now different trade shows nationwide.

**[02:22:29.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002835)  
In NCR, we have one to two every month.

**[02:22:33.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002836)  
We also have globally a lot of trade shows.

**[02:22:37.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002837)  
In fact, we've been aggressive now in joining.

**[02:22:39.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002838)  
That's where exports are up.

**[02:22:40.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002839)  
All these trade shows, po, are free.

**[02:22:43.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002840)  
And then we continue to provide also customers from cafes, hotels, different grocery supermarkets.

**[02:22:55.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002841)  
We invite them.

**[02:22:56.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002842)  
Actually, we engage a company that brings in all of these buyers.

**[02:23:00.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002843)  
Then we've also invited companies like Jollibee, companies that are big that can buy from the

**[02:23:09.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002844)  
local manufacturers.

**[02:23:12.359] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002845)  
How about in terms of accessing the local market?

**[02:23:14.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002846)  
Uh, local, um...

**[02:23:16.439] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002847)  
Local, let's say, supermarkets?

**[02:23:17.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002848)  
Yes, we do.

**[02:23:18.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002849)  
In fact, SM, Robinsons, Pure Gold, so many of the groceries and supermarkets are also

**[02:23:25.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002850)  
buying from these.

**[02:23:26.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002851)  
And also yung mga cafes, yung mga nagbabottle din, mga caterers.

**[02:23:31.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002861)  
When they buy, are they, hindi ba, pagka meron tayong local retailers, yung ating local retailers, very prohibitive yung pagpasok ng MSMEs para ma-display yung produkto sa kanila.

**[02:23:47.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002862)  
They charge listing fees, they charge kung ano-anong fee na sinisingil na lang sa invoice mo.

**[02:23:52.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002863)  
Kaya minsan, ang nangyayari, yung MSME, sa dami ng sinisingil ng local retailers, pagtingin na lang ang invoice nila, wala na silang kinikita.

**[02:24:02.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002864)  
Okay, let's say for SM, there's a different rate for MSMEs. In fact, they provided in all their malls an area where MSMEs can sell their products.

**[02:24:11.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002865)  
So, mas mababa po yung rent, very much lower. And they also provide different, kasi marami silang supermarkets, groceries.

**[02:24:20.479] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002866)  
Lalo na for MSMEs?

**[02:24:22.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002867)  
Totally none, kasi they also employ people. So, they need to charge.

**[02:24:27.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002868)  
Pero anong nangyayari po? If you're a local manufacturer, you're an MSME, and then you're, magdi-display kayo sa local retailer,

**[02:24:40.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002869)  
kayo rin, ikaw rin ang nagbabayad ng, what do you call this, yung mga merchandisers.

**[02:24:48.399] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002870)  
Ah.

**[02:25:01.319] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002874)  
But what the others do then, let's say I'm an MSME, I can manufacture for a brand. I'm a subcontractor now.

**[02:25:19.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002876)

**[02:25:21.720] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002877)

**[02:25:22.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002878)

**[02:25:23.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002879)

**[02:25:31.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002880)

**[02:25:36.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002881)

**[02:25:46.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002882)

**[02:25:59.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002883)

**[02:26:06.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002884)

277 MSMEs

**[03:05:57.520] DTI halal-program respondent Bianca (functional attribution)** (asr-003679)

**[03:06:16.600] DTI halal-program respondent Bianca (functional attribution)** (asr-003682)

**[03:06:19.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003683)

**[03:06:29.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003684)

**[03:07:20.720] DTI halal-program respondent Bianca (functional attribution)** (asr-003693)

**[03:07:22.399] DTI halal-program respondent Bianca (functional attribution)** (asr-003694)

**[03:07:30.359] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003695)

**[03:07:33.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003696)

**[03:07:42.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003697)

**[03:07:48.399] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003698)

**[03:07:52.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003699)

**[03:08:01.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003700)

**[03:08:10.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003701)

**[03:08:20.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003702)  
and we need more...

**[03:08:22.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003703)

**[03:08:29.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003704)

**[03:08:35.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003705)

**[03:08:37.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003706)

**[03:08:40.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003707)  
So, malaki din po yung potential ng halal products dito.

**[03:08:43.840] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003708)

**[03:08:50.479] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003709)  
Kasi yun po yung importante.

**[03:08:52.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003710)

**[03:08:59.760] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003711)

**[03:09:04.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-003712)  
But for this year, nakikita natin that this is really a huge potential for export po.

**[03:09:11.439] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003713)  
Definitely, Madam Chair.

**[03:09:13.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003714)

**[03:09:28.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003718)

**[03:09:32.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003719)

**[03:30:12.199] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-004213)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, and good afternoon to our colleagues and DTI family. Firstly, I would like to express my support to the budget of the DTI and whatever adjustment we could have.

**[03:30:26.319] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-004214)

**[03:30:40.239] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-004215)  
So we need to hear the explanation, of course, from the DTI. Bakit sobrang layo na ng budget natin compared to the GAA of 2026, Madam Chair? Anong nangyari?

Good afternoon, Honorable Congressman. Actually, we did not expect also na bumaba yung budget because we have a lot of programs,

**[03:31:09.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004217)  
and there's also a lot of things that we need to do to be able to contribute to providing jobs and also to provide the industries with a push para at least makausa din sila.

**[03:31:27.160] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004218)  
So this is why we are here, to ask for additional budget for the three main mandates of DTI, which is investment promotion, consumer protection, and fair trade.

**[03:31:39.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004219)  
And also the MSME development. So yun ang kailangan po natin.

**[03:31:45.239] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004220)  
Because we need to, the only way for us to be able to push also these programs is for us to be able to have the budget that we need.

**[03:31:53.359] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004221)  
Without this budget, then it will be difficult for us to also push the products that we need to export or that we need to sell locally.

**[03:32:01.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004222)  
And then we also need to enforce and to make sure that the products that are being sold in the market pass through the standards of DTI.

**[03:32:11.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004224)  
And we want to encourage more investors to come into the Philippines, but again, we also need budget to be able to promote this.

**[03:32:19.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004225)  
Even if our numbers are up, our exports are up, also our investments are up, but then there's also the Middle East crisis that also contributed to the challenge that we are facing now.

**[03:32:32.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004226)  
But again, tuloy-tuloy pa rin ang mga programa ng DTI.

**[03:32:41.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004230)

**[03:32:52.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-004231)

**[04:14:41.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005024)

**[04:14:58.840] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005027)

**[04:15:11.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005028)  
So, Madam Chair, if I may ask, these wages would be competitive internationally? Okay. Just wanted to have that confirmation. Is there anything in the current studies or projections about energy generation to be able to compensate for the industries that will be built for Paxilica?

**[04:15:35.720] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005029)  
Honorable Chair and Honorable Representative, yung pong usapan nga po natin is,

**[04:15:40.880] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005030)  
uh, uh,

**[04:15:41.319] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005033)  
build your own roads, bring your own power, and secure and reuse your own water. Pagdating ko sa energy, noting that hindi naman po data center talaga yung itatayo doon sa, doon sa New Clark City, but advanced manufacturing, meron na rin pong assessment na ginawa si BCDA.

**[04:16:08.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005034)  
Does that mean, when you say secure your own water, does that mean rainwater?

**[04:16:11.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005035)  
Harvesting, desalination, ah, dam, maybe?

**[04:16:16.560] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005036)  
Thank you, Honorable Representative. Yes po, pareho yung dalawang yun. Yung nakikita kong isang plano is to secure surface water, rainwater. In particular, kitang kita natin nitong nakaraang dalawang linggo na tuloy-tuloy yung pagulan, nasasayang ko yung tubig na dumadaloy doon sa mga rivers na malapit doon. For example, kayang-kaya kong lagyan ng impounding station.

**[04:16:40.920] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005037)  
Kagaya ho ng ginawa ng BCDA, for example, dito sa BGC, kaya hindi bumabaha kasi may track record doon ang BCDA dito. And pangalawa ho, tama ho yung sinabi ninyo, isa rin ho sa discussion natin, yung desalination facility. In fact, doon sa isang kausap po ng BCDA, it's a Middle East company, sinabi niya magtatayo siya ng desalination facility sa Subic, tapos ipapipe niya all the way to Clark.

**[04:17:09.840] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005038)  
Atin, ang layo niyan, sorry, in English po sinabi natin, that's so many kilometers away. Ang sagot po nung kausap ng BCDA is, actually that's just 75 kilometers. You're thinking in terms of what you are doing now in the Philippines, but in the Middle East, we are piping thousands of kilometers. That 75 kilometers is just like piping from one village to the next village.

**[04:17:35.760] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005039)  
So, yun po. Both of your, what you have mentioned as possible solutions.

**[04:17:42.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005040)  
Madam Chair, the reason why I ask these questions is, ang takot ng mga kababayan natin ay, tataas ba ang electricity bill code dahil sa Paxilica? Yung tubig na kailangan natin, are they going to consume it all?

**[04:17:55.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005041)

**[04:18:20.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005043)

This is the only time I've ever...

**[04:19:24.680] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-005048)

**[04:23:24.800] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005163)

**[04:23:28.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005165)

**[04:23:30.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005166)

**[04:23:32.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005167)

**[04:23:35.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005168)

**[04:23:37.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005169)

**[04:23:39.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005170)

**[04:23:40.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005171)

**[04:23:43.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005172)

**[04:23:44.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005173)

**[04:23:48.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005175)

**[04:23:50.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005176)

**[04:23:52.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005177)

**[04:23:54.399] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005178)

**[04:23:56.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005179)

**[04:23:59.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005180)

**[04:24:01.159] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005181)

**[04:24:03.520] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005182)

**[04:24:04.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005183)

**[04:24:06.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005184)

Chair. Good afternoon, and

**[04:28:36.680] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005319)

**[04:28:38.319] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005320)  
Abaca is not only our province’s

**[04:28:40.239] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005321)  
major product, but also

**[04:28:42.279] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005322)

**[04:28:44.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005323)  
and has long been part

**[04:28:46.199] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005324)  
of our identity.

**[04:28:50.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005325)  
However,

**[04:28:50.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005326)  
we are seeing a concern decline

**[04:28:52.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005327)  
in production.

**[04:28:54.680] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005328)  
Nationally, abaca production

**[04:28:56.479] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005329)  
declined from about

**[04:28:58.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005330)  
70,209

**[04:28:59.680] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005331)  
metric tons in 2019

**[04:29:04.479] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005332)  
to 55,816 metric tons

**[04:29:09.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005336)  
in 2025.

**[04:29:12.319] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005337)  
Or around 22.7

**[04:29:14.080] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005338)  
percent in

**[04:29:15.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005339)  
Katanduanis, the decline

**[04:29:18.359] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005340)  
was much sharper from

**[04:29:20.159] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005341)  
20,371.43

**[04:29:24.239] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005342)  
metric ton

**[04:29:24.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005343)  
in 2024

**[04:29:27.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005344)  
to only

**[04:29:28.080] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005345)  
11,256

**[04:29:30.279] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005346)  
metric ton in 2025.

**[04:29:33.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005347)  
At

**[04:29:33.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005348)  
44.7% drop

**[04:29:35.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005349)  
despite this, Katanduanis still

**[04:29:37.800] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005350)  
accounted for

**[04:29:39.479] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005351)  
20.2% of the national

**[04:29:42.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005352)  
production and

**[04:29:43.640] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005353)  
87.5

**[04:29:47.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005354)  
fiber production in 2025. May I ask DTI: has the department studied this decline and its impact on the entire abaca and fiber industry? How is this decline affecting the entire abaca value chain?

**[04:30:11.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005355)  
Not only farmers, but also processors, traders, handicraft and furniture makers, exporters, and other downstream enterprises that depend on a steady supply of abaca.

**[04:30:26.720] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005356)

**[04:30:38.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005357)  
Madam Chair.

**[04:30:39.640] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005358)  
Yes, Honorable Congressman. Thank you for your question.

**[04:30:42.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005359)  
We provide market access and also assistance for all the products that are manufactured in the Philippines.

**[04:30:49.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005360)  
There are 18 regional offices nationwide and 1,400 negocio centers where these companies or these individuals can go and ask us for assistance, possibilities, and opportunities, including the loan program.

**[04:31:05.119] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005361)  
I will defer to Asec. Grace Baluyan.

**[04:31:08.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005362)  
Asik Grace Baluyen is one of the regional offices regarding ABACA, but for all the products of the Philippines, we find market access for this, whether locally or globally, but they just have to actually inform us or engage with us.

**[04:31:23.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005363)  
We have a lot of opportunities for them if they want to penetrate the strong local market or they want to penetrate, of course, the huge global market.

**[04:31:32.880] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005364)  
We have different foreign trade attaches all over the world.

**[04:31:41.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005365)  
but for abaca in the particular region, I defer to Asec. Grace Baluyan regarding this.

**[04:31:52.680] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005366)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[04:31:56.840] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005367)  
We do provide interventions to our abaca sector, but only at the MSME level.

**[04:32:04.760] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005368)  
So they are able to avail of the regular programs of DTI.

**[04:32:10.279] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005369)  
Particularly,

**[04:32:11.760] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005370)  
as my secretary mentioned, access to market, product development, under OTOP, under the SSF, under the Negotiation Center Services, sir.

**[04:32:22.279] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005371)  
However, there's also a program that has not been funded for a long, long time, but we continue to pursue by using our partnerships at the local level.

**[04:32:32.960] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005372)  
We have what we call the Industry Cluster Enhancement Program, of which priority industries are enrolled.

**[04:32:41.080] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005373)  
One of these is abaca, which falls under wearables and homestyle.

**[04:32:48.840] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005374)  
We have a National Industry Coordinator, which helps the regions craft a roadmap for industry development.

**[04:32:58.159] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005375)  
Unfortunately, sir, we don't have resources to really put in additional programs that are industry-wide in scope.

**[04:33:06.920] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005376)  
But we continue to sustain the existing programs.

**[04:33:10.680] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005377)  
So we continue to sustain the existing platforms of collaboration, the associations at the provincial level and the regional level.

**[04:33:16.760] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005378)  
So how I wish, sir, this will be also provided consideration, so that we can continue to pursue promoting industries, particularly those that are community-based, those that are in the agribusiness.

**[04:33:33.561] DTI Assistant Secretary Grace F. Baluyan** (asr-005379)  
Thank you, sir.

**[04:33:35.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005380)  
Madam Chair,

**[04:33:36.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005381)  
Ah, halos 47.7% yung decline.

**[04:33:41.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005385)  
So medyo nakakalarma naman po ito.

**[04:33:44.480] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005386)  
Tama po, sir.

**[04:33:47.279] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005387)  
It's alarming.

**[04:33:50.359] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005388)  
At the local level, sir, we have organizations, industry organizations.

**[04:33:54.959] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005389)  
And in some areas, there are councils.

**[04:33:57.719] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005390)  
So the councils become the platform for collaboration.

**[04:34:01.199] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005391)

**[04:34:06.639] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005392)  
So if, with your permission, sir, we can give you a report

**[04:34:12.879] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005394)  
on what we can do. In fact, one concern as well, sir, is not just the

**[04:34:18.639] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005395)  
dwindling raw material supply but also markets; the market has shrunk to some extent.

**[04:34:27.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005396)  
Oo nga kasi, alam mo naman, yung abaca ngayon napakababa ng presyo. So kaya kawawa naman yung ating mga, ika nga, farmers, yung abacaleros natin. So kasi itong ganitong decline na 44.7%,

**[04:34:45.719] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005397)  
eh medyo dapat magkaroon tayo rito ng appropriate na intervention dito, kasi medyo lalo na sa amin sa Bicol,

**[04:34:56.039] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005398)  
Catanduanes, ito po ang aming produkto. So papaano natin ipo-promote yung produkto namin kung ganito naman

**[04:35:06.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005399)  
kakababa? Isa po iyan sa malaking katanungan

**[04:35:13.160] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005400)  
namin doon po kasi abaca capital kami na turingan pero parang wala namang nangyayari. So

**[04:35:22.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005401)  
isa po iyan, Madam Chair, Madam Secretary, sa isa sa mga problema namin doon.

**[04:35:32.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005402)  
Honorable Congressman, if I may add, the production of abaca is with the DA, but idi-discuss ko

**[04:35:39.279] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005403)  
po itong concern ninyo kay Secretary Kiko. But the abaca fiber exports po in 2025 is up by 25%, 21.7 million. So maybe yung mga kumpanya po na nasa regions or nasa province ninyo can coordinate with the Export Marketing Bureau so that

**[04:36:01.039] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005404)  
we

**[04:36:01.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005405)  
can assist them to find markets both locally

**[04:36:04.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005406)  
and globally para at least po, maybe yung ibang market nila, sometimes

**[04:36:09.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005407)  
kasi, di ba, like

**[04:36:09.799] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005408)  
sa negosyo, some markets close but other markets open.

**[04:36:13.439] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005409)  
Especially with the free trade agreements that we have as this administration will go down in history as the most free trade agreement.

**[04:36:21.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005410)  
Pwede po natin ipasok sa ibang mga markets na nag-open po that have the preferential tariff.

**[04:36:27.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005411)  
In fact, ito po yung ginagawa natin dito sa DTI ng ibang mga products po that maybe hindi na ganun kalakas sa market dito.

**[04:36:37.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005412)  
So, we have the world as the market now.

**[04:36:40.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005413)  
So, marami po tayong pwedeng pagbentahan po ng mga products.

**[04:36:44.639] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005414)  
So, we will closely coordinate po with your province regarding this.

**[04:36:50.920] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005415)  
Because the fact that abaca fiber exports are up, that means that there is a global market for abaca po.

**[04:36:58.879] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005416)  
And then maybe for local, we can also find other markets for your products din po.

**[04:37:04.199] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005417)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:37:05.719] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005418)  
Madam Secretary.

**[04:37:10.400] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005419)

**[04:37:15.320] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005420)

**[04:37:26.680] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005421)

**[04:37:32.719] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005422)

**[04:37:34.199] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005423)

**[04:38:06.959] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005427)

**[04:38:20.680] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005428)

So

coordinating with this

may mai-present yung

**[04:39:35.320] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005447)  
Honorable Congressman,

**[04:39:36.561] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005448)  
actually,

**[04:39:39.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005449)  
I suggest that yung mga negosyante

**[04:39:41.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005450)  
in your province and all the provinces of the country should, um, go to the different regional offices nationwide.

**[04:39:48.600] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005451)  
Meron po tayong 18 regional offices in the 18 regions and 1,400 Negosyo Centers nationwide.

**[04:39:55.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005452)  
Nandun po yung mga possibilities and opportunities that the department has for each and every negosyo.

**[04:40:03.718] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005453)  
But they need to go there po so that they will know kung anong offering po ang meron ang Department of Trade para sa kanila.

**[04:40:11.240] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005454)  
For those that want to export,

**[04:40:12.958] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005455)  
we have the EMB,

**[04:40:14.638] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005456)  
the Export Marketing Bureau,

**[04:40:17.638] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005457)  
that's also within the DTI,

**[04:40:19.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005458)  
that will actually guide them every step of the way.

**[04:40:23.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005459)  
And then for those that want to sell locally,

**[04:40:25.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005460)  
we also have the trade shows po nationwide.

**[04:40:28.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005461)  
Lahat po ng trade shows locally and globally under the Department of Trade is free.

**[04:40:33.400] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005462)  
So it's best for them po to have a close,

**[04:40:36.878] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005463)  
since mga negosyante po sila,

**[04:40:38.560] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005464)  
it's best for them to have a close relationship.

**[04:40:41.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005465)  
with the regional offices of your province or of every province and the different

**[04:40:45.718] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005466)  
Negosyo Centers para pag may bagong offerings, which we normally always have,

**[04:40:50.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005467)  
puwede po i-relay sa kanila.

**[04:40:52.360] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005468)  
And then mayroon din kaming pautang para sa kanila, whether they want to sell their products locally or globally.

**[04:40:58.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005469)  
Mayroon din po tayong mga market access, meaning kung gusto nilang magbenta sa malls,

**[04:41:02.638] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005470)  
gusto nilang sumali sa trade shows, or they also want to sell to a certain company,

**[04:41:08.120] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005471)  
puwede po naming i-facilitate iyon.

**[04:41:10.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005473)  
We've done that for so many companies, and we continue to do that.

**[04:41:15.040] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005474)  
So, we just have to inform the businessmen, or the businesses, or the SMEs,

**[04:41:20.680] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-005475)  
that they must continue to closely engage with the Department of Trade and Industry.

**[04:41:26.718] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005476)  
Oo, kasi dapat itong mga ito sinusuportahan natin kasi diyan tayo nagsisimula, yung mga small.

**[04:41:33.878] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005477)  
So ang problema nga, kahit nga itong sa OTOP natin, pag iyan

**[04:41:37.480] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005478)

**[04:41:39.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005479)

**[04:41:45.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005480)

So sabi ko, dapat ma-address na ito, hindi na tayo pabalik-balik sa ganoong problema palagi, kasi naging mayor po ako, nine years po akong nag-mayor, kaya experience ko na ito eh.

**[04:42:32.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-005485)

It's best for them to coordinate

**[04:51:13.400] APECO President and CEO Atty. Gil G. Taway IV** (asr-005687)

**[05:26:18.558] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006170)

**[05:26:28.798] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006173)

**[05:26:37.480] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006174)

**[05:29:07.958] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-006198)  
Yes, actually, from the DPWH ito. Yusek Perry can explain it further.

**[05:29:15.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006199)

**[05:29:21.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006201)  
Madam Chair, thank you for the question, Representative Tinio.

**[05:29:25.718] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006202)  
So, yung total amount po is 5.1 billion pesos.

**[05:29:30.760] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006203)  
Ito po yung utang natin kay Toyota, Mitsubishi, Denso, Manly Plastics, Valery, F-Tech Philippines, SMRC Automotive Technology, Roberts Automotive, tsaka...

**[05:29:44.840] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006204)  
So both the program participants at the model level, so yun pong mga banyaga, pati rin po yung mga local na mga nagsusupply ng mga auto parts.

**[05:29:55.558] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006205)  
So ang total amount po ng arirages natin, ang utang natin is 5.156 billion pesos. Tama po kayo na una po meron kaming 125 million pesos yata na nasa budget namin tapos nilagay sa unprogrammed allocation na Vito po.

**[05:30:14.600] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006206)  
Tapos ngayon dahil sa kailangan talagang pagkakautang na po natin ito, so kailangan talagang bayaran dahil nag-deliver na po sila ng kanilang mga commitments.

**[05:30:25.240] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006207)  
So kukunin po yun, na-identify po. Pwede po kasi mag-identify ka ng savings from other government agencies. Ang nakita po ay may savings po si DPWH. Tapos so yun po yung pagkukuha na ng 5.1 billion pesos. Yung naging savings po nila for 2020-2025.

**[05:30:52.080] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006208)  
So savings of 5 billion.

**[05:30:55.520] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006209)  
5.1 billion from DPWH 2025, yun ang gagamitin na pambayad sa CARS. So augmentation from savings essentially. Provided may item para doon. Meron bang item para doon?

**[05:31:11.200] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006210)  
Meron pa rin po item na nakalagay doon sa GAAN natin. Meron po kasing meron tayong nakalagay doon na parang maliit lang po na implementation of the CARS program. I think 10 million pesos.

**[05:31:25.440] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006211)  
So may paglalagyan pa rin kung kung baga na bahay. May bahay na tinatawag. May bahay po tayo na paglalagyan.

**[05:31:30.638] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006212)  
Okay. Pero ito pala ang nakaka, ngayon ko lang nalaman, savings may 5 billion. Ah, okay.

**[05:31:38.638] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006213)  
Sorry po.

**[05:31:39.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006214)  
Baka, sige baka.

**[05:31:39.958] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006215)

**[05:31:47.718] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006216)

**[05:31:54.280] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006217)  
[unclear],

**[05:31:55.080] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006218)

**[05:31:57.120] DTI Undersecretary Ceferino S. Rodolfo** (asr-006219)  
para sa mga incentives nila.

**[05:31:59.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006219-split-2)  
Okay. So thank you for that clarification. Magtatanong din tayo sa DBM at sa DPWH regarding this, no?

**[05:32:05.718] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006220)  
Okay. Yung third na lang na concern natin with regard to the tariffs imposed by the US, yung 12.5% dahil daw nag, the Philippines imports from countries with forced labor.

**[05:32:25.638] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006221)  
At ang sabi ng DTI, as far as I know, mga 40%, up to 40% ng Philippine exports affected dito. Tama po ba?

**[05:32:35.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006222)  
Could you elaborate? Bakit tayo, akala ko ba magkaibigan ang Pilipinas at US pero pinatawan ng 12.5%? Yung Malaysia, Indonesia, parang 10% lang yata? Diba? Tayo 12 pa?

**[05:32:55.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006223)  
12.5. Please explain the consequences, anong epekto sa mga exporters natin? Sino-sino ang mga ito at anong ginagawa nyo?

**[05:33:06.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006224)  
Dahil syempre, dihado, magiging mas mahal ang mga produktong Pilipino in highly competitive export markets.

**[05:33:13.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-006225)  
Yes, actually, Kong, for the exporters, we also try to find, in case may problem sila pagpasok ng goods sa US, we find other markets for them.

**[05:33:23.200] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-006226)  
We have different foreign trade attachés globally. So, nakakahanap po kami ng market para sa kanila and also locally. I'll forward you, Kong, Honorable Kong, to the trade negotiator, to our chief negotiator to discuss yung 12.5%.

**[05:33:42.080] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-006227)  
Usec. Allan?

**[05:33:44.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006228)  
Ano ba na-explain nyo? Bakit, ano yung sabihin nito? Like, my understanding, mainly this is...

**[05:33:52.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006229)  
meant to penalize countries that import from China, among others. Diba ganun? O, huwag na tayo maglokohan. Yun yun eh. Sige, please.

**[05:34:05.638] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006230)  
Thank you, ma'am, and good afternoon, Your Honors. Number one, with respect to the imposition of tariffs by the United States, I think in terms of context, Po, Your Honor, we have to understand that when President Trump,

**[05:34:23.120] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006231)  
assumed office, the policy of the United States has been to impose unilateral tariffs. So, basically, the direction is really for them to hit countries wherein they have deficit. But, of course, the first unilateral tariffs was declared unconstitutional by the US Supreme Court, and so they resorted again to another basis by which they can impose unilateral tariffs.

**[05:34:47.558] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006232)  
After that, they imposed Section 122 tariffs, which can only last for 150 days.

**[05:34:53.878] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006233)  
And then, after the expiration of Section 1 to 2, now they initiated the investigation under Section 301, and that's the basis by which they have imposed the 12.5% tariffs for, I think, 67 countries, including the Philippines, and for, I think, only around 10 countries, 10%.

**[05:35:15.200] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006234)  
Now, the basis of Section 301 tariffs under the Trade Regulation Act of the United States, Your Honor, is actually...

**[05:35:23.558] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006235)  
premise on the fact that countries or economies trading with the United States has no law that bans or prohibits the importation of goods which are made or results of forced labor.

**[05:35:39.600] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006236)  
So, that's the basis. So, in other words, it doesn't mean that a particular country or economy has a weak regime when it comes to combating, for example, forced labor.

**[05:35:52.878] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006237)  
So, in the case of the Philippines, we issued a joint administrative order institutionalizing the mechanism by which we can address that particular concern of the United States.

**[05:36:07.958] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006238)  
And the same is under evaluation now of the US for purposes of at least compliance to their Section 301 investigation.

**[05:36:18.120] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006239)  
So, iyon po ngayon iyong status ngayon.

**[05:36:20.680] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006240)  
Negotiations are still ongoing.

**[05:36:23.120] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006241)  
In fact, itong recent issuance po ng JAO, under evaluation.

**[05:36:26.600] DTI Undersecretary Allan B. Gepty** (asr-006242)
